补充 · prod_mk_fst_snd 定理
theorem prod_mk_fst_snd (X : Prod Nat Nat) :
    @prod_mk Nat Nat (Prod.fst Nat Nat X) (Prod.snd Nat Nat X) = X :=
  match X with
  | prod_mk a b => Eq.refl (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b)
补充 · intRel_cancel_den_left 定理
theorem intRel_cancel_den_left (a b a' b' m : Nat)
    (h : intRel (mulRep (den m) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
                (mulRep (den m) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b'))) :
    intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') :=
  rat_cross a b a' b' m
    (intRel_trans
      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den m))
      (mulRep (den m) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') (den m))
      (intRel_of_eq
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den m))
        (mulRep (den m) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
        (mulRep_comm a b (Nat.succ m) Nat.zero))
      (intRel_trans
        (mulRep (den m) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
        (mulRep (den m) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b'))
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') (den m))
        h
        (intRel_of_eq
          (mulRep (den m) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b'))
          (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') (den m))
          (@Eq.symm.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
            (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') (den m))
            (mulRep (den m) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b'))
            (mulRep_comm a' b' (Nat.succ m) Nat.zero)))))
补充 · mulRep_zero_left_form 定理
theorem mulRep_zero_left_form (s c d : Nat) :
    mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero s) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) =
      @prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul s d) (myMul s c) :=
  prod_mk_congr
    (Nat.add (myMul Nat.zero c) (myMul s d)) (myMul s d)
    (Nat.add (myMul Nat.zero d) (myMul s c)) (myMul s c)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul Nat.zero c) (myMul s d))
      (Nat.add Nat.zero (myMul s d))
      (myMul s d)
      (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x (myMul s d)) (zero_mul c))
      (zero_add (myMul s d)))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul Nat.zero d) (myMul s c))
      (Nat.add Nat.zero (myMul s c))
      (myMul s c)
      (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x (myMul s c)) (zero_mul d))
      (zero_add (myMul s c)))
补充 · intRel_mul_zero_left 定理
theorem intRel_mul_zero_left (s a b : Nat) :
    intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero s) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul s b) (myMul s a)) :=
  intRel_of_eq
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero s) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul s b) (myMul s a))
    (mulRep_zero_left_form s a b)
补充 · intRel_mul_zero_mid 定理
theorem intRel_mul_zero_mid (s a b a' b' : Nat)
    (h : intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero s) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero s) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b'))) :
    intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul s b) (myMul s a))
           (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul s b') (myMul s a')) :=
  intRel_trans
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul s b) (myMul s a))
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero s) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul s b') (myMul s a'))
    (intRel_of_eq
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul s b) (myMul s a))
      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero s) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
      (@Eq.symm.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero s) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul s b) (myMul s a))
        (mulRep_zero_left_form s a b)))
    (intRel_trans
      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero s) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero s) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b'))
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul s b') (myMul s a'))
      h
      (intRel_of_eq
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero s) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b'))
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul s b') (myMul s a'))
        (mulRep_zero_left_form s a' b')))
补充 · mul_shift_left 定理
theorem mul_shift_left (a b m : Nat) :
    myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ m) =
      Nat.add (myMul (Nat.succ m) a) (myMul (Nat.succ m) b) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ m))
    (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b (Nat.succ m)))
    (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.succ m) a) (myMul (Nat.succ m) b))
    (mul_add_right a b (Nat.succ m))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b (Nat.succ m)))
      (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.succ m) a) (myMul b (Nat.succ m)))
      (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.succ m) a) (myMul (Nat.succ m) b))
      (congrArg.{1}
        (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x (myMul b (Nat.succ m)))
        (myMul_comm a (Nat.succ m)))
      (congrArg.{1}
        (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add (myMul (Nat.succ m) a) x)
        (myMul_comm b (Nat.succ m))))
补充 · intRel_cancel_neg_den 定理
theorem intRel_cancel_neg_den (a b a' b' m : Nat)
    (h : intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero (Nat.succ m)) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero (Nat.succ m)) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b'))) :
    intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') :=
  @Eq.symm.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add a' b)
    (Nat.add a b')
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add a' b)
      (Nat.add b a')
      (Nat.add a b')
      (add_comm a' b)
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add b a')
        (Nat.add b' a)
        (Nat.add a b')
        (mul_cancel_pos m (Nat.add b a') (Nat.add b' a)
          (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
            (myMul (Nat.add b a') (Nat.succ m))
            (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.succ m) b) (myMul (Nat.succ m) a'))
            (myMul (Nat.add b' a) (Nat.succ m))
            (mul_shift_left b a' m)
            (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
              (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.succ m) b) (myMul (Nat.succ m) a'))
              (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.succ m) b') (myMul (Nat.succ m) a))
              (myMul (Nat.add b' a) (Nat.succ m))
              (intRel_mul_zero_mid (Nat.succ m) a b a' b' h)
              (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
                (myMul (Nat.add b' a) (Nat.succ m))
                (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.succ m) b') (myMul (Nat.succ m) a))
                (mul_shift_left b' a m)))))
        (add_comm b' a)))
补充 · intRel_to_zero_left 定理
theorem intRel_to_zero_left (p q k : Nat) (h : q = Nat.add p (Nat.succ k)) :
    intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero (Nat.succ k)) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add p (Nat.succ k))
    q
    (Nat.add Nat.zero q)
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat q (Nat.add p (Nat.succ k)) h)
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add Nat.zero q) q (zero_add q))
补充 · intRel_to_den_right 定理
theorem intRel_to_den_right (p q k : Nat) (h : p = Nat.add q (Nat.succ k)) :
    intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (den k) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add p Nat.zero)
    p
    (Nat.add (Nat.succ k) q)
    (add_zero p)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      p
      (Nat.add q (Nat.succ k))
      (Nat.add (Nat.succ k) q)
      h
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (Nat.succ k) q)
        (Nat.add q (Nat.succ k))
        (add_comm (Nat.succ k) q)))
补充 · intRel_cancel_nonzero_pos 定理
theorem intRel_cancel_nonzero_pos (p q c d e f : Nat)
    (hpq : LeNat p q)
    (hne : ¬ intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero))
    (h : intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f))) :
    intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) :=
  Or.elim
    (fun (heq : p = q) =>
      @False.elim
        (intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f))
        (hne (intRel_zero_of_eq p q heq)))
    (fun (hlt : LeNat (Nat.succ p) q) =>
      Exists.elim (leNat_exists_add (Nat.succ p) q hlt)
        (fun (k : Nat) (hk : q = Nat.add (Nat.succ p) k) =>
          intRel_cancel_neg_den c d e f k
            (intRel_trans
              (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero (Nat.succ k)) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
              (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
              (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero (Nat.succ k)) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f))
              (intRel_symm
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero (Nat.succ k)) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
                (mulRep_wd_gen (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
                  (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero (Nat.succ k))
                  (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)
                  (intRel_to_zero_left p q k
                    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
                      q
                      (Nat.add (Nat.succ p) k)
                      (Nat.add p (Nat.succ k))
                      hk
                      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
                        (Nat.add (Nat.succ p) k)
                        (Nat.succ (Nat.add p k))
                        (Nat.add p (Nat.succ k))
                        (succ_add p k)
                        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
                          (Nat.add p (Nat.succ k))
                          (Nat.succ (Nat.add p k))
                          (add_succ p k)))))))
              (intRel_trans
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f))
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero (Nat.succ k)) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f))
                h
                (mulRep_wd_gen (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
                  (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero (Nat.succ k))
                  (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)
                  (intRel_to_zero_left p q k
                    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
                      q
                      (Nat.add (Nat.succ p) k)
                      (Nat.add p (Nat.succ k))
                      hk
                      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
                        (Nat.add (Nat.succ p) k)
                        (Nat.succ (Nat.add p k))
                        (Nat.add p (Nat.succ k))
                        (succ_add p k)
                        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
                          (Nat.add p (Nat.succ k))
                          (Nat.succ (Nat.add p k))
                          (add_succ p k))))))))))
    (leNat_eq_or_succ p q hpq)
补充 · intRel_cancel_nonzero_neg 定理
theorem intRel_cancel_nonzero_neg (p q c d e f : Nat)
    (hqp : LeNat q p)
    (hne : ¬ intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero))
    (h : intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f))) :
    intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) :=
  Or.elim
    (fun (heq : q = p) =>
      @False.elim
        (intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f))
        (hne (intRel_zero_of_eq p q (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat q p heq))))
    (fun (hlt : LeNat (Nat.succ q) p) =>
      Exists.elim (leNat_exists_add (Nat.succ q) p hlt)
        (fun (k : Nat) (hk : p = Nat.add (Nat.succ q) k) =>
          intRel_cancel_den_left c d e f k
            (intRel_trans
              (mulRep (den k) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
              (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
              (mulRep (den k) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f))
              (intRel_symm
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
                (mulRep (den k) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
                (mulRep_wd_gen (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (den k)
                  (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)
                  (intRel_to_den_right p q k
                    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
                      p
                      (Nat.add (Nat.succ q) k)
                      (Nat.add q (Nat.succ k))
                      hk
                      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
                        (Nat.add (Nat.succ q) k)
                        (Nat.succ (Nat.add q k))
                        (Nat.add q (Nat.succ k))
                        (succ_add q k)
                        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
                          (Nat.add q (Nat.succ k))
                          (Nat.succ (Nat.add q k))
                          (add_succ q k)))))))
              (intRel_trans
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f))
                (mulRep (den k) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f))
                h
                (mulRep_wd_gen (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (den k)
                  (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)
                  (intRel_to_den_right p q k
                    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
                      p
                      (Nat.add (Nat.succ q) k)
                      (Nat.add q (Nat.succ k))
                      hk
                      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
                        (Nat.add (Nat.succ q) k)
                        (Nat.succ (Nat.add q k))
                        (Nat.add q (Nat.succ k))
                        (succ_add q k)
                        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
                          (Nat.add q (Nat.succ k))
                          (Nat.succ (Nat.add q k))
                          (add_succ q k))))))))))
    (leNat_eq_or_succ q p hqp)
补充 · mulRep_zero_pair 定理
theorem mulRep_zero_pair (c d : Nat) :
    mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) =
      @prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero :=
  prod_mk_congr
    (Nat.add (myMul Nat.zero c) (myMul Nat.zero d)) Nat.zero
    (Nat.add (myMul Nat.zero d) (myMul Nat.zero c)) Nat.zero
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul Nat.zero c) (myMul Nat.zero d))
      (Nat.add Nat.zero Nat.zero)
      Nat.zero
      (add_congr (zero_mul c) (zero_mul d))
      (zero_add Nat.zero))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul Nat.zero d) (myMul Nat.zero c))
      (Nat.add Nat.zero Nat.zero)
      Nat.zero
      (add_congr (zero_mul d) (zero_mul c))
      (zero_add Nat.zero))
补充 · mulRep_den_zero 定理
theorem mulRep_den_zero (p q : Nat) :
    intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (den Nat.zero))
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) :=
  mulRep_den (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) Nat.zero
补充 · intRel_zero_iff 定理
theorem intRel_zero_iff (p q : Nat) :
    intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero) ↔ p = q :=
  Iff.intro
    (fun (h : Nat.add p Nat.zero = Nat.add Nat.zero q) =>
      @Eq.trans.{1} Nat p (Nat.add p Nat.zero) q
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add p Nat.zero) p (add_zero p))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add p Nat.zero) (Nat.add Nat.zero q) q
          h
          (zero_add q)))
    (fun (h : p = q) => intRel_zero_of_eq p q h)
补充 · intRel_cancel_den 定理
theorem intRel_cancel_den (X Y : Prod Nat Nat) (m : Nat)
    (h : intRel (mulRep X (den m)) (mulRep Y (den m))) :
    intRel X Y :=
  rat_cross (Prod.fst Nat Nat X) (Prod.snd Nat Nat X)
    (Prod.fst Nat Nat Y) (Prod.snd Nat Nat Y) m h
补充 · intRel_cancel_nonzero_gen 定理
theorem intRel_cancel_nonzero_gen (p q : Nat) (X Y : Prod Nat Nat)
    (hne : ¬ intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero))
    (h : intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) X)
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) Y)) :
    intRel X Y :=
  intRel_trans X
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat (Prod.fst Nat Nat X) (Prod.snd Nat Nat X))
    Y
    (intRel_symm
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat (Prod.fst Nat Nat X) (Prod.snd Nat Nat X))
      X
      (intRel_of_eq
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat (Prod.fst Nat Nat X) (Prod.snd Nat Nat X))
        X (prod_mk_fst_snd X)))
    (intRel_trans
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat (Prod.fst Nat Nat X) (Prod.snd Nat Nat X))
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat (Prod.fst Nat Nat Y) (Prod.snd Nat Nat Y))
      Y
      (intRel_cancel_nonzero p q
        (Prod.fst Nat Nat X) (Prod.snd Nat Nat X)
        (Prod.fst Nat Nat Y) (Prod.snd Nat Nat Y)
        hne
        (intRel_trans
          (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
            (@prod_mk Nat Nat (Prod.fst Nat Nat X) (Prod.snd Nat Nat X)))
          (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) X)
          (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
            (@prod_mk Nat Nat (Prod.fst Nat Nat Y) (Prod.snd Nat Nat Y)))
          (intRel_symm
            (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) X)
            (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
              (@prod_mk Nat Nat (Prod.fst Nat Nat X) (Prod.snd Nat Nat X)))
            (intRel_of_eq
              (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
                (@prod_mk Nat Nat (Prod.fst Nat Nat X) (Prod.snd Nat Nat X)))
              (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) X)
              (@congrArg.{1} (Prod Nat Nat) (Prod Nat Nat)
                (@prod_mk Nat Nat (Prod.fst Nat Nat X) (Prod.snd Nat Nat X))
                X
                (fun (Z : Prod Nat Nat) => mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) Z)
                (prod_mk_fst_snd X))))
          (intRel_trans
            (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) X)
            (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) Y)
            (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
              (@prod_mk Nat Nat (Prod.fst Nat Nat Y) (Prod.snd Nat Nat Y)))
            h
            (intRel_of_eq
              (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) Y)
              (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
                (@prod_mk Nat Nat (Prod.fst Nat Nat Y) (Prod.snd Nat Nat Y)))
              (@congrArg.{1} (Prod Nat Nat) (Prod Nat Nat)
                Y
                (@prod_mk Nat Nat (Prod.fst Nat Nat Y) (Prod.snd Nat Nat Y))
                (fun (Z : Prod Nat Nat) => mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) Z)
                (@Eq.symm.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
                  (@prod_mk Nat Nat (Prod.fst Nat Nat Y) (Prod.snd Nat Nat Y))
                  Y (prod_mk_fst_snd Y)))))))
      (intRel_of_eq
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat (Prod.fst Nat Nat Y) (Prod.snd Nat Nat Y))
        Y (prod_mk_fst_snd Y)))
补充 · mulRep_den_ratDenRep_swap 定理
theorem mulRep_den_ratDenRep_swap (N : Prod Nat Nat) (m n : Nat) :
    intRel (mulRep N (den (ratDenRep m n)))
      (mulRep (mulRep N (den n)) (den m)) :=
  intRel_trans
    (mulRep N (den (ratDenRep m n)))
    (mulRep (mulRep N (den m)) (den n))
    (mulRep (mulRep N (den n)) (den m))
    (mulRep_den_ratDenRep N m n)
    (intRel_symm
      (mulRep (mulRep N (den n)) (den m))
      (mulRep (mulRep N (den m)) (den n))
      (mulRep_den_swap_gen N n m))
补充 · mulRep_num_den_swap 定理
theorem mulRep_num_den_swap (p q c d m n : Nat) :
    intRel
      (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
        (den (ratDenRep m n)))
      (mulRep
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
          (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den n)))
        (den m)) :=
  intRel_trans
    (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
      (den (ratDenRep m n)))
    (mulRep
      (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) (den n))
      (den m))
    (mulRep
      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den n)))
      (den m))
    (mulRep_den_ratDenRep_swap
      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) m n)
    (mulRep_wd_gen
      (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) (den n))
      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den n)))
      (den m)
      (mulRep_assoc_gen (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den n)))
补充 · ratMul_cancel_aux 定理
theorem ratMul_cancel_aux (p q m c d n e f k : Nat)
    (hne : ¬ intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero))
    (h : ratRel
      (ratMulRep (qrep p q m) (qrep c d n))
      (ratMulRep (qrep p q m) (qrep e f k))) :
    ratRel (qrep c d n) (qrep e f k) :=
  intRel_cancel_nonzero_gen p q
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den k))
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den n))
    hne
    (intRel_trans
      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den k)))
      (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) (den k))
      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den n)))
      (intRel_symm
        (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) (den k))
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
          (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den k)))
        (mulRep_assoc_gen (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den k)))
      (intRel_trans
        (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) (den k))
        (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)) (den n))
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
          (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den n)))
        (intRel_cancel_den
          (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) (den k))
          (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)) (den n))
          m
          (intRel_trans
            (mulRep (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) (den k)) (den m))
            (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) (den (ratDenRep m k)))
            (mulRep (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)) (den n)) (den m))
            (intRel_symm
              (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) (den (ratDenRep m k)))
              (mulRep (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) (den k)) (den m))
              (intRel_trans
                (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) (den (ratDenRep m k)))
                (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
                  (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den k))) (den m))
                (mulRep (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) (den k)) (den m))
                (mulRep_num_den_swap p q c d m k)
                (mulRep_wd_gen
                  (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
                    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den k)))
                  (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) (den k))
                  (den m)
                  (intRel_symm
                    (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) (den k))
                    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
                      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den k)))
                    (mulRep_assoc_gen (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den k))))))
            (intRel_trans
              (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) (den (ratDenRep m k)))
              (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)) (den (ratDenRep m n)))
              (mulRep (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)) (den n)) (den m))
              h
              (intRel_trans
                (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)) (den (ratDenRep m n)))
                (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
                  (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den n))) (den m))
                (mulRep (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)) (den n)) (den m))
                (mulRep_num_den_swap p q e f m n)
                (mulRep_wd_gen
                  (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
                    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den n)))
                  (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)) (den n))
                  (den m)
                  (intRel_symm
                    (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)) (den n))
                    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q)
                      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den n)))
                    (mulRep_assoc_gen (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den n))))))))
        (mulRep_assoc_gen (@prod_mk Nat Nat p q) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den n))))

III.7 · 本章第 6 / 11 单元 · 全书第 145 / 171 单元

145导数的和与差：先让乘法对加法分配

Unit 145 — Sums and Differences of Derivatives: Distributing Multiplication Over Addition

12 个定理 · 12 道练习 · 36 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

两条曲线在 a 点各有导数 L 与 M，把纵坐标逐点相加，新曲线在这一点的导数是不是 L + M？差商的形状几乎是现成的：两条乘积等式 (x - a) · q x ~ f x - f a 与 (x - a) · r x ~ g x - g a 相加，右边正好是 (f x + g x) - (f a + g a)。挡路的只有左边一步：要把 (x - a) · q x + (x - a) · r x 收成 (x - a) · (q x + r x)。

练习 1ratMul_add定理附录解答

课程的 ℚ 是整数对的商，乘法与加法各自先通分再运算。乘法对加法分配在代表元层不是定义性相等：两边的分母相差一个因子，交叉相乘之后要比较四个单项式的次序。前面的单元没用到这条分配律，它一直空着；导数的和第一次需要它。

证明把两边约到同一个分母。约掉公共因子之后，剩下的等式只关于四个单项式：左边按 ac、ae、bd、bf 排列，右边按 ac、bd、ae、bf 排列，只差中间两项。ℕ 的乘法交换、结合与分配负责对齐每一项，add_shift 负责交换中间两项。

边界：函数取 ratRep → ratRep，停在有理代表元层；不构造倒数，不新增公理，也不碰 kernel/front。常数倍还要一条乘法对第二个因子的同余，留到后面的单元。

乘法对加法分配

把 x、y、z 依次写成 (a - b)/(m + 1)、(c - d)/(n + 1)、(e - f)/(k + 1)。左边的分母是 (m + 1)(n + 1)(k + 1)，右边的分母是 (m + 1)²(n + 1)(k + 1)，多出一个 m + 1。交叉相乘并约掉公共因子之后，要证的是四个单项式的重排：ac(k+1) + ae(n+1) + bd(k+1) + bf(n+1) 与 ac(k+1) + bd(k+1) + ae(n+1) + bf(n+1) 相等。

theorem ratMul_add (x y z : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep x (ratAddRep y z))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep x z)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把 x、y、z 拆成字面三元组；两边分别用 mulRep_den_form 化成同一形状的交叉乘积；约掉公共因子 (m+1)(n+1)(k+1) 后，用 ℕ 的交换、结合、分配把四个单项式逐项对齐，再用 add_shift 交换中间两项。

提示 2目标形态

ratRel (ratMulRep x (ratAddRep y z)) (ratAddRep (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep x z))。

提示 3关键件

mulRep_den_form · mul_cancel_pos · myMul_assoc · myMul_comm · mul_add · mul_add_right · add_shift · intRel_trans。

查看解答 定理 · 4 行
theorem ratMul_add (x y z : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep x (ratAddRep y z))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep x z)) :=
  ratMul_add_pack x y z
练习 2ratMul_sub定理附录解答

分配律的减法版本

x · (y - z) 里的减法按定义是 y + (-z)。分配之后第二项是 x · (-z)，它等于 -(x · z)：两边按 qrep 展开后，第一格都是 a·d + b·c 取负，第二格都是 a·c + b·d 取负，只是两项的次序不同。把这一项换掉，x · (y - z) 就收成 x · y - x · z。

theorem ratMul_sub (x y z : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep x (ratSubRep y z))
      (ratSubRep (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep x z)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把 y - z 展开成 y + (-z)；用 ratMul_add 分配；再用 ratMul_neg 把 x·(-z) 换成 -(x·z)；最后按减法的定义把 x·y + -(x·z) 收回 x·y - x·z。

提示 2目标形态

ratRel (ratMulRep x (ratSubRep y z)) (ratSubRep (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep x z))。

提示 3关键件

ratMul_add · ratMul_neg · ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen · ratRel_trans_gen。

查看解答 定理 · 10 行
theorem ratMul_sub (x y z : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep x (ratSubRep y z))
      (ratSubRep (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep x z)) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (ratMulRep x (ratSubRep y z))
    (ratAddRep (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep x (ratNegRep z)))
    (ratSubRep (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep x z))
    (ratMul_add x y (ratNegRep z))
    (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep x (ratNegRep z))
      (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x z)) (ratMul_neg x z))
练习 3ratAdd_sub_sub定理附录解答

两个差相加

(p - q) + (r - s) 可以并成一个差 (p + r) - (q + s)。四个项不直接混在一起：先把两个差各补一个中间项，p - q 与 (p + r) - (r + q) 相等，r - s 与 (r + q) - (s + q) 相等。补好之后两个差共享中间项 r + q，ratSubRep_add 把它消掉，剩下的正是 (p + r) - (q + s)。

theorem ratAdd_sub_sub (p q r s : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratAddRep (ratSubRep p q) (ratSubRep r s))
      (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep q s)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把第一个差 p - q 换成 (p+r) - (r+q)（减去同一个加数），把第二个差 r - s 换成 (r+q) - (s+q)；两个补好的差共享中间项 r+q，交给 ratSubRep_add 消去，再把 s+q 换成 q+s。

提示 2目标形态

ratRel ((p - q) + (r - s)) ((p + r) - (q + s))。

提示 3关键件

ratSubRep_add_cancel · ratSubRep_add · ratSubRep_wd · ratAdd_comm · ratAddRep_wd_gen · ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen。

查看解答 定理 · 41 行
theorem ratAdd_sub_sub (p q r s : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratAddRep (ratSubRep p q) (ratSubRep r s))
      (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep q s)) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (ratAddRep (ratSubRep p q) (ratSubRep r s))
    (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep r q))
               (ratSubRep r s))
    (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep q s))
    (ratAddRep_wd_gen
      (ratSubRep p q) (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep r q))
      (ratSubRep r s)
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratSubRep p q)
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep q r))
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep r q))
        (ratRel_symm (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep q r))
          (ratSubRep p q) (ratSubRep_add_cancel p q r))
        (ratSubRep_wd (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep p r)
          (ratAddRep q r) (ratAddRep r q)
          (ratRel_refl (ratAddRep p r)) (ratAdd_comm q r))))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep r q))
                 (ratSubRep r s))
      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep r q))
                 (ratSubRep (ratAddRep r q) (ratAddRep s q)))
      (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep q s))
      (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep r q))
        (ratSubRep r s)
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep r q) (ratAddRep s q))
        (ratRel_symm (ratSubRep (ratAddRep r q) (ratAddRep s q))
          (ratSubRep r s) (ratSubRep_add_cancel r s q)))
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep r q))
                   (ratSubRep (ratAddRep r q) (ratAddRep s q)))
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep s q))
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep q s))
        (ratSubRep_add (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep r q) (ratAddRep s q))
        (ratSubRep_wd (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep p r)
          (ratAddRep s q) (ratAddRep q s)
          (ratRel_refl (ratAddRep p r)) (ratAdd_comm s q))))
练习 4isSlope_add定理附录解答

斜率函数逐点相加

f 与 g 的斜率函数分别是 q 与 r。逐点把 (x - a) · (q x + r x) 用 ratMul_add 拆成两份乘积，两条乘积等式分别换成 f x - f a 与 g x - g a，最后用 ratAdd_sub_sub 并成 (f x + g x) - (f a + g a)。

theorem isSlope_add (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (q r : ratRep → ratRep) (hq : IsSlope f a q) (hr : IsSlope g a r) :
    IsSlope (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) a
      (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (q x) (r x)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

固定 x 与 x ≠ a；把 (x-a)·(q x + r x) 分配成两份乘积；每一份用对应的斜率等式换成函数值之差；再用 ratAdd_sub_sub 把两个差并成一个差。

提示 2目标形态

IsSlope (fun x => f x + g x) a (fun x => q x + r x)。

提示 3关键件

ratMul_add · ratAddRep_wd_gen · ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen · ratAdd_sub_sub · ratRel_trans_gen。

查看解答 定理 · 35 行
theorem isSlope_add (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (q r : ratRep → ratRep) (hq : IsSlope f a q) (hr : IsSlope g a r) :
    IsSlope (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) a
      (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (q x) (r x)) :=
  fun (x : ratRep) (hx : ¬ ratRel x a) =>
    ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (ratAddRep (q x) (r x)))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (q x))
                 (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (r x)))
      (ratSubRep (ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) (ratAddRep (f a) (g a)))
      (ratMul_add (ratSubRep x a) (q x) (r x))
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (q x))
                   (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (r x)))
        (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
                   (ratSubRep (g x) (g a)))
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) (ratAddRep (f a) (g a)))
        (ratRel_trans_gen
          (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (q x))
                     (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (r x)))
          (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
                     (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (r x)))
          (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
                     (ratSubRep (g x) (g a)))
          (ratAddRep_wd_gen
            (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (q x))
            (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
            (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (r x))
            (hq x hx))
          (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen
            (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
            (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (r x))
            (ratSubRep (g x) (g a))
            (hr x hx)))
        (ratAdd_sub_sub (f x) (f a) (g x) (g a)))
练习 5hasDerivAt_add定理附录解答

两个导数各给一个斜率函数与一个极限值。和的斜率函数取 q + r，和的极限值取 L + M：前半交给上一条，后半交给函数极限的加法律。

theorem hasDerivAt_add (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a L M : ratRep)
    (hf : HasDerivAt f a L) (hg : HasDerivAt g a M) :
    HasDerivAt (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) a
      (ratAddRep L M) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

拆开两条导数，各得一个斜率函数与一条极限；把两个斜率函数逐点相加，用 isSlope_add 与前一半拼起来；极限交给 tendsToAt_add。

提示 2目标形态

HasDerivAt (fun x => f x + g x) a (L + M)。

提示 3关键件

Exists.elim · isSlope_add · tendsToAt_add · And.left · And.right。

查看解答 定理 · 14 行
theorem hasDerivAt_add (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a L M : ratRep)
    (hf : HasDerivAt f a L) (hg : HasDerivAt g a M) :
    HasDerivAt (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) a
      (ratAddRep L M) :=
  Exists.elim hf
    (fun (q : ratRep → ratRep)
        (hq : IsSlope f a q ∧ TendsToAt q a L) =>
      Exists.elim hg
        (fun (r : ratRep → ratRep)
            (hr : IsSlope g a r ∧ TendsToAt r a M) =>
          Exists.intro (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (q x) (r x))
            (And.intro
              (isSlope_add f g a q r (And.left hq) (And.left hr))
              (tendsToAt_add q r a L M (And.right hq) (And.right hr)))))
练习 6differentiableAt_add定理附录解答

可导的存在性逐层搬运：和的导数就是两个导数值的和。

theorem differentiableAt_add (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hf : DifferentiableAt f a) (hg : DifferentiableAt g a) :
    DifferentiableAt (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) a :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

拆开两个可导假设，各取一个导数值；把两个值相加作为和的导数，交给 hasDerivAt_add。

提示 2目标形态

DifferentiableAt (fun x => f x + g x) a。

提示 3关键件

Exists.elim · Exists.intro · hasDerivAt_add。

查看解答 定理 · 9 行
theorem differentiableAt_add (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hf : DifferentiableAt f a) (hg : DifferentiableAt g a) :
    DifferentiableAt (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) a :=
  Exists.elim hf
    (fun (L : ratRep) (hL : HasDerivAt f a L) =>
      Exists.elim hg
        (fun (M : ratRep) (hM : HasDerivAt g a M) =>
          Exists.intro (ratAddRep L M)
            (hasDerivAt_add f g a L M hL hM)))
练习 7isSlope_sub定理附录解答

斜率函数逐点相减

取负已经备好：isSlope_neg 把斜率函数整体取负。把 f - g 读成 f + (-g)，它的斜率函数就是 q + (-r)，也就是 q - r。

theorem isSlope_sub (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (q r : ratRep → ratRep) (hq : IsSlope f a q) (hr : IsSlope g a r) :
    IsSlope (fun (x : ratRep) => ratSubRep (f x) (g x)) a
      (fun (x : ratRep) => ratSubRep (q x) (r x)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把 f - g 写成 f + (-g)；对 -g 用 isSlope_neg 把斜率函数取负；再把两个斜率函数交给 isSlope_add。

提示 2目标形态

IsSlope (fun x => f x - g x) a (fun x => q x - r x)。

提示 3关键件

isSlope_neg · isSlope_add。

查看解答 定理 · 6 行
theorem isSlope_sub (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (q r : ratRep → ratRep) (hq : IsSlope f a q) (hr : IsSlope g a r) :
    IsSlope (fun (x : ratRep) => ratSubRep (f x) (g x)) a
      (fun (x : ratRep) => ratSubRep (q x) (r x)) :=
  isSlope_add f (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (g x)) a q
    (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (r x)) hq (isSlope_neg g a r hr)

theorem hasDerivAt_sub (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a L M : ratRep)
    (hf : HasDerivAt f a L) (hg : HasDerivAt g a M) :
    HasDerivAt (fun (x : ratRep) => ratSubRep (f x) (g x)) a
      (ratSubRep L M) :=
  hasDerivAt_add f (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (g x)) a L
    (ratNegRep M) hf (hasDerivAt_neg g a M hg)

导数的和与差：先让乘法对加法分配

练习 1 · ratMul_add 定理
theorem ratMul_add (x y z : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep x (ratAddRep y z))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep x z)) :=
  ratMul_add_pack x y z
练习 2 · ratMul_sub 定理
theorem ratMul_sub (x y z : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep x (ratSubRep y z))
      (ratSubRep (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep x z)) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (ratMulRep x (ratSubRep y z))
    (ratAddRep (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep x (ratNegRep z)))
    (ratSubRep (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep x z))
    (ratMul_add x y (ratNegRep z))
    (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep x (ratNegRep z))
      (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x z)) (ratMul_neg x z))
练习 3 · ratAdd_sub_sub 定理
theorem ratAdd_sub_sub (p q r s : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratAddRep (ratSubRep p q) (ratSubRep r s))
      (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep q s)) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (ratAddRep (ratSubRep p q) (ratSubRep r s))
    (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep r q))
               (ratSubRep r s))
    (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep q s))
    (ratAddRep_wd_gen
      (ratSubRep p q) (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep r q))
      (ratSubRep r s)
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratSubRep p q)
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep q r))
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep r q))
        (ratRel_symm (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep q r))
          (ratSubRep p q) (ratSubRep_add_cancel p q r))
        (ratSubRep_wd (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep p r)
          (ratAddRep q r) (ratAddRep r q)
          (ratRel_refl (ratAddRep p r)) (ratAdd_comm q r))))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep r q))
                 (ratSubRep r s))
      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep r q))
                 (ratSubRep (ratAddRep r q) (ratAddRep s q)))
      (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep q s))
      (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep r q))
        (ratSubRep r s)
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep r q) (ratAddRep s q))
        (ratRel_symm (ratSubRep (ratAddRep r q) (ratAddRep s q))
          (ratSubRep r s) (ratSubRep_add_cancel r s q)))
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep r q))
                   (ratSubRep (ratAddRep r q) (ratAddRep s q)))
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep s q))
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep q s))
        (ratSubRep_add (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep r q) (ratAddRep s q))
        (ratSubRep_wd (ratAddRep p r) (ratAddRep p r)
          (ratAddRep s q) (ratAddRep q s)
          (ratRel_refl (ratAddRep p r)) (ratAdd_comm s q))))
练习 4 · isSlope_add 定理
theorem isSlope_add (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (q r : ratRep → ratRep) (hq : IsSlope f a q) (hr : IsSlope g a r) :
    IsSlope (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) a
      (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (q x) (r x)) :=
  fun (x : ratRep) (hx : ¬ ratRel x a) =>
    ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (ratAddRep (q x) (r x)))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (q x))
                 (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (r x)))
      (ratSubRep (ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) (ratAddRep (f a) (g a)))
      (ratMul_add (ratSubRep x a) (q x) (r x))
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (q x))
                   (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (r x)))
        (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
                   (ratSubRep (g x) (g a)))
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) (ratAddRep (f a) (g a)))
        (ratRel_trans_gen
          (ratAddRep (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (q x))
                     (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (r x)))
          (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
                     (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (r x)))
          (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
                     (ratSubRep (g x) (g a)))
          (ratAddRep_wd_gen
            (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (q x))
            (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
            (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (r x))
            (hq x hx))
          (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen
            (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
            (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (r x))
            (ratSubRep (g x) (g a))
            (hr x hx)))
        (ratAdd_sub_sub (f x) (f a) (g x) (g a)))
练习 5 · hasDerivAt_add 定理
theorem hasDerivAt_add (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a L M : ratRep)
    (hf : HasDerivAt f a L) (hg : HasDerivAt g a M) :
    HasDerivAt (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) a
      (ratAddRep L M) :=
  Exists.elim hf
    (fun (q : ratRep → ratRep)
        (hq : IsSlope f a q ∧ TendsToAt q a L) =>
      Exists.elim hg
        (fun (r : ratRep → ratRep)
            (hr : IsSlope g a r ∧ TendsToAt r a M) =>
          Exists.intro (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (q x) (r x))
            (And.intro
              (isSlope_add f g a q r (And.left hq) (And.left hr))
              (tendsToAt_add q r a L M (And.right hq) (And.right hr)))))
练习 6 · differentiableAt_add 定理
theorem differentiableAt_add (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hf : DifferentiableAt f a) (hg : DifferentiableAt g a) :
    DifferentiableAt (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) a :=
  Exists.elim hf
    (fun (L : ratRep) (hL : HasDerivAt f a L) =>
      Exists.elim hg
        (fun (M : ratRep) (hM : HasDerivAt g a M) =>
          Exists.intro (ratAddRep L M)
            (hasDerivAt_add f g a L M hL hM)))
练习 7 · isSlope_sub 定理
theorem isSlope_sub (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (q r : ratRep → ratRep) (hq : IsSlope f a q) (hr : IsSlope g a r) :
    IsSlope (fun (x : ratRep) => ratSubRep (f x) (g x)) a
      (fun (x : ratRep) => ratSubRep (q x) (r x)) :=
  isSlope_add f (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (g x)) a q
    (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (r x)) hq (isSlope_neg g a r hr)
练习 8 · hasDerivAt_sub 定理
theorem hasDerivAt_sub (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a L M : ratRep)
    (hf : HasDerivAt f a L) (hg : HasDerivAt g a M) :
    HasDerivAt (fun (x : ratRep) => ratSubRep (f x) (g x)) a
      (ratSubRep L M) :=
  hasDerivAt_add f (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (g x)) a L
    (ratNegRep M) hf (hasDerivAt_neg g a M hg)
练习 9 · differentiableAt_sub 定理
theorem differentiableAt_sub (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hf : DifferentiableAt f a) (hg : DifferentiableAt g a) :
    DifferentiableAt (fun (x : ratRep) => ratSubRep (f x) (g x)) a :=
  differentiableAt_add f (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (g x)) a hf
    (differentiableAt_neg g a hg)

    ratOneRep ratOneRep (hasDerivAt_id a) (hasDerivAt_id a)

补充 · mul_rotate 定理
theorem mul_rotate (p q s r : Nat) :
    myMul (myMul p (myMul q s)) r = myMul (myMul p q) (myMul r s) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (myMul (myMul p (myMul q s)) r)
    (myMul p (myMul (myMul q s) r))
    (myMul (myMul p q) (myMul r s))
    (myMul_assoc p (myMul q s) r)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (myMul p (myMul (myMul q s) r))
      (myMul p (myMul q (myMul s r)))
      (myMul (myMul p q) (myMul r s))
      (congrArg.{1} (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul p Z) (myMul_assoc q s r))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (myMul p (myMul q (myMul s r)))
        (myMul p (myMul q (myMul r s)))
        (myMul (myMul p q) (myMul r s))
        (congrArg.{1} (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul p (myMul q Z)) (myMul_comm s r))
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
          (myMul (myMul p q) (myMul r s))
          (myMul p (myMul q (myMul r s)))
          (myMul_assoc p q (myMul r s)))))
补充 · sum4_mul 定理
theorem sum4_mul (x1 x2 x3 x4 c : Nat) :
    myMul (Nat.add (Nat.add x1 x2) (Nat.add x3 x4)) c =
      Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul x1 c) (myMul x2 c))
              (Nat.add (myMul x3 c) (myMul x4 c)) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (myMul (Nat.add (Nat.add x1 x2) (Nat.add x3 x4)) c)
    (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add x1 x2) c) (myMul (Nat.add x3 x4) c))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul x1 c) (myMul x2 c))
             (Nat.add (myMul x3 c) (myMul x4 c)))
    (mul_add_right (Nat.add x1 x2) (Nat.add x3 x4) c)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add x1 x2) c) (myMul (Nat.add x3 x4) c))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul x1 c) (myMul x2 c))
               (myMul (Nat.add x3 x4) c))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul x1 c) (myMul x2 c))
               (Nat.add (myMul x3 c) (myMul x4 c)))
      (congrArg.{1}
        (fun (Z : Nat) => Nat.add Z (myMul (Nat.add x3 x4) c))
        (mul_add_right x1 x2 c))
      (congrArg.{1}
        (fun (Z : Nat) =>
          Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul x1 c) (myMul x2 c)) Z)
        (mul_add_right x3 x4 c)))
补充 · term1 定理
theorem term1 (a c m k : Nat) :
    myMul (myMul a (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (Nat.succ m) =
      myMul (myMul a c) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (myMul (myMul a (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (Nat.succ m))
    (myMul (myMul a c) (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ k)))
    (myMul (myMul a c) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
    (mul_rotate a c (Nat.succ k) (Nat.succ m))
    (congrArg.{1} (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul (myMul a c) Z)
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k))
        (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ k)) (ratDenRep_succ m k)))

theorem a_expand (a b c d e f k n : Nat) :
    Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ k)) (myMul e (Nat.succ n))))
            (myMul b (Nat.add (myMul d (Nat.succ k)) (myMul f (Nat.succ n)))) =
      Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (myMul a (myMul e (Nat.succ n))))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (myMul b (myMul f (Nat.succ n)))) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ k)) (myMul e (Nat.succ n))))
             (myMul b (Nat.add (myMul d (Nat.succ k)) (myMul f (Nat.succ n)))))
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul a (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (myMul a (myMul e (Nat.succ n))))
      (myMul b (Nat.add (myMul d (Nat.succ k)) (myMul f (Nat.succ n)))))
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul a (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (myMul a (myMul e (Nat.succ n))))
      (Nat.add (myMul b (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (myMul b (myMul f (Nat.succ n)))))
    (congrArg.{1}
      (fun (Z : Nat) =>
        Nat.add Z (myMul b (Nat.add (myMul d (Nat.succ k)) (myMul f (Nat.succ n)))))
      (mul_add a (myMul c (Nat.succ k)) (myMul e (Nat.succ n))))
    (congrArg.{1}
      (fun (Z : Nat) =>
        Nat.add
          (Nat.add (myMul a (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (myMul a (myMul e (Nat.succ n))))
          Z)
      (mul_add b (myMul d (Nat.succ k)) (myMul f (Nat.succ n))))

补充 · a_main 定理
theorem a_main (a b m c d n e f k : Nat) :
    myMul
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ k)) (myMul e (Nat.succ n))))
               (myMul b (Nat.add (myMul d (Nat.succ k)) (myMul f (Nat.succ n)))))
      (Nat.succ m) =
    Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
            (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a e) (myMul b f)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (myMul
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ k)) (myMul e (Nat.succ n))))
               (myMul b (Nat.add (myMul d (Nat.succ k)) (myMul f (Nat.succ n)))))
      (Nat.succ m))
    (myMul
      (Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (myMul a (myMul e (Nat.succ n))))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (myMul b (myMul f (Nat.succ n)))))
      (Nat.succ m))
    (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
             (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a e) (myMul b f)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
    (congrArg.{1} (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul Z (Nat.succ m))
      (a_expand a b c d e f k n))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (myMul
        (Nat.add
          (Nat.add (myMul a (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (myMul a (myMul e (Nat.succ n))))
          (Nat.add (myMul b (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (myMul b (myMul f (Nat.succ n)))))
        (Nat.succ m))
      (Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (Nat.succ m))
                 (myMul (myMul a (myMul e (Nat.succ n))) (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul (myMul b (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (Nat.succ m))
                 (myMul (myMul b (myMul f (Nat.succ n))) (Nat.succ m))))
      (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
               (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a e) (myMul b f)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
      (sum4_mul (myMul a (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (myMul a (myMul e (Nat.succ n)))
        (myMul b (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (myMul b (myMul f (Nat.succ n)))
        (Nat.succ m))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add
          (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (Nat.succ m))
                   (myMul (myMul a (myMul e (Nat.succ n))) (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.add (myMul (myMul b (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (Nat.succ m))
                   (myMul (myMul b (myMul f (Nat.succ n))) (Nat.succ m))))
        (Nat.add
          (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a c) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                   (myMul (myMul a e) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
          (Nat.add (myMul (myMul b d) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                   (myMul (myMul b f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))))
        (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                 (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a e) (myMul b f)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
        (a_terms a b m c d n e f k)
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add
            (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a c) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                     (myMul (myMul a e) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
            (Nat.add (myMul (myMul b d) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                     (myMul (myMul b f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))))
          (Nat.add
            (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a c) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                     (myMul (myMul b d) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k))))
            (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a e) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
                     (myMul (myMul b f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))))
          (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                   (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a e) (myMul b f)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
          (add_shift (myMul (myMul a c) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
            (myMul (myMul b d) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
            (myMul (myMul a e) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
            (myMul (myMul b f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
          (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
            (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                     (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a e) (myMul b f)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
            (Nat.add
              (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a c) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                       (myMul (myMul b d) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k))))
              (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a e) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
                       (myMul (myMul b f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))))
            (a_P a b m c d n e f k)))))
补充 · b_expand 定理
theorem b_expand (a b c d e f k n : Nat) :
    Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.add (myMul d (Nat.succ k)) (myMul f (Nat.succ n))))
            (myMul b (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ k)) (myMul e (Nat.succ n)))) =
      Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (myMul a (myMul f (Nat.succ n))))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (myMul b (myMul e (Nat.succ n)))) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.add (myMul d (Nat.succ k)) (myMul f (Nat.succ n))))
             (myMul b (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ k)) (myMul e (Nat.succ n)))))
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul a (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (myMul a (myMul f (Nat.succ n))))
      (myMul b (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ k)) (myMul e (Nat.succ n)))))
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul a (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (myMul a (myMul f (Nat.succ n))))
      (Nat.add (myMul b (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (myMul b (myMul e (Nat.succ n)))))
    (congrArg.{1}
      (fun (Z : Nat) =>
        Nat.add Z (myMul b (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ k)) (myMul e (Nat.succ n)))))
      (mul_add a (myMul d (Nat.succ k)) (myMul f (Nat.succ n))))
    (congrArg.{1}
      (fun (Z : Nat) =>
        Nat.add
          (Nat.add (myMul a (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (myMul a (myMul f (Nat.succ n))))
          Z)
      (mul_add b (myMul c (Nat.succ k)) (myMul e (Nat.succ n))))
补充 · b_P 定理
theorem b_P (a b m c d n e f k : Nat) :
    Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
            (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b e)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))) =

        (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a d) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                 (myMul (myMul b c) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k))))

  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
             (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b e)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a d) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
               (myMul (myMul b c) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k))))
      (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b e)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a d) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
               (myMul (myMul b c) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k))))
      (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
               (myMul (myMul b e) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))))
    (congrArg.{1}
      (fun (Z : Nat) =>
        Nat.add Z (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b e)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
      (mul_add_right (myMul a d) (myMul b c) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k))))
    (congrArg.{1}
      (fun (Z : Nat) =>
        Nat.add
          (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a d) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                   (myMul (myMul b c) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k))))
          Z)
      (mul_add_right (myMul a f) (myMul b e) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))

theorem b_terms (a b m c d n e f k : Nat) :
    Nat.add

               (myMul (myMul a f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
      (Nat.add (myMul (myMul b c) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))

                   (myMul (myMul b (myMul e (Nat.succ n))) (Nat.succ m))))

        (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (Nat.succ m))
                 (myMul (myMul a (myMul f (Nat.succ n))) (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a d) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                 (myMul (myMul a (myMul f (Nat.succ n))) (Nat.succ m)))

                 (myMul (myMul a f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))

          (term2 a f m n))))

                   (myMul (myMul a f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))

补充 · b_main 定理
theorem b_main (a b m c d n e f k : Nat) :
    myMul
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.add (myMul d (Nat.succ k)) (myMul f (Nat.succ n))))
               (myMul b (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ k)) (myMul e (Nat.succ n)))))

            (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b e)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))) :=

               (myMul b (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ k)) (myMul e (Nat.succ n)))))
      (Nat.succ m))
    (myMul
      (Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (myMul a (myMul f (Nat.succ n))))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (myMul b (myMul e (Nat.succ n)))))
      (Nat.succ m))
    (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
             (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b e)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
    (congrArg.{1} (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul Z (Nat.succ m))
      (b_expand a b c d e f k n))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (myMul
        (Nat.add
          (Nat.add (myMul a (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (myMul a (myMul f (Nat.succ n))))
          (Nat.add (myMul b (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (myMul b (myMul e (Nat.succ n)))))
        (Nat.succ m))
      (Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (Nat.succ m))
                 (myMul (myMul a (myMul f (Nat.succ n))) (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul (myMul b (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (Nat.succ m))
                 (myMul (myMul b (myMul e (Nat.succ n))) (Nat.succ m))))
      (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
               (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b e)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
      (sum4_mul (myMul a (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (myMul a (myMul f (Nat.succ n)))
        (myMul b (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (myMul b (myMul e (Nat.succ n)))
        (Nat.succ m))

          (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a (myMul d (Nat.succ k))) (Nat.succ m))

          (Nat.add (myMul (myMul b (myMul c (Nat.succ k))) (Nat.succ m))
                   (myMul (myMul b (myMul e (Nat.succ n))) (Nat.succ m))))
        (Nat.add
          (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a d) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                   (myMul (myMul a f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
          (Nat.add (myMul (myMul b c) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                   (myMul (myMul b e) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))))
        (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                 (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b e)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
        (b_terms a b m c d n e f k)
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat

                     (myMul (myMul b e) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))))
          (Nat.add
            (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a d) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                     (myMul (myMul b c) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k))))
            (Nat.add (myMul (myMul a f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
                     (myMul (myMul b e) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))))
          (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))

补充 · denD_R 定理
theorem denD_R (m n k : Nat) :
    Nat.succ (ratDenRep (ratDenRep m n) (ratDenRep m k)) =
      myMul (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n))
            (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ k)) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.succ (ratDenRep (ratDenRep m n) (ratDenRep m k)))
    (myMul (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
    (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n))
           (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ k)))
    (ratDenRep_succ (ratDenRep m n) (ratDenRep m k))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (myMul (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
      (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
      (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n))
             (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ k)))
      (congrArg.{1}
        (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul Z (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
        (ratDenRep_succ m n))
      (congrArg.{1}
        (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n)) Z)
        (ratDenRep_succ m k)))
补充 · denD_L 定理
theorem denD_L (m n k : Nat) :
    Nat.succ (ratDenRep m (ratDenRep n k)) =

    (myMul (Nat.succ m) (myMul (Nat.succ n) (Nat.succ k)))
    (ratDenRep_succ m (ratDenRep n k))
    (congrArg.{1}
      (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul (Nat.succ m) Z)
      (ratDenRep_succ n k))
补充 · mul_mid_swap 定理
theorem mul_mid_swap (q r s : Nat) :
    myMul r (myMul q s) = myMul q (myMul r s) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (myMul r (myMul q s))
    (myMul (myMul r q) s)
    (myMul q (myMul r s))
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul (myMul r q) s) (myMul r (myMul q s)) (myMul_assoc r q s))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (myMul (myMul r q) s)
      (myMul (myMul q r) s)
      (myMul q (myMul r s))
      (congrArg.{1} (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul Z s) (myMul_comm r q))
      (myMul_assoc q r s))
补充 · den_rotate 定理
theorem den_rotate (p q r s : Nat) :
    myMul p (myMul (myMul q r) (myMul q s)) =
      myMul (myMul p q) (myMul q (myMul r s)) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (myMul p (myMul (myMul q r) (myMul q s)))
    (myMul (myMul p (myMul q r)) (myMul q s))
    (myMul (myMul p q) (myMul q (myMul r s)))
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul (myMul p (myMul q r)) (myMul q s))
      (myMul p (myMul (myMul q r) (myMul q s)))
      (myMul_assoc p (myMul q r) (myMul q s)))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (myMul (myMul p (myMul q r)) (myMul q s))
      (myMul (myMul (myMul p q) r) (myMul q s))
      (myMul (myMul p q) (myMul q (myMul r s)))
      (congrArg.{1} (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul Z (myMul q s))
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul (myMul p q) r) (myMul p (myMul q r))
          (myMul_assoc p q r)))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (myMul (myMul (myMul p q) r) (myMul q s))
        (myMul (myMul p q) (myMul r (myMul q s)))
        (myMul (myMul p q) (myMul q (myMul r s)))
        (myMul_assoc (myMul p q) r (myMul q s))
        (congrArg.{1} (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul (myMul p q) Z)
          (mul_mid_swap q r s))))
补充 · crossA 定理
theorem crossA (x m n k : Nat) :
    myMul x (Nat.succ (ratDenRep (ratDenRep m n) (ratDenRep m k))) =
      myMul (myMul x (Nat.succ m)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m (ratDenRep n k))) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (myMul x (Nat.succ (ratDenRep (ratDenRep m n) (ratDenRep m k))))
    (myMul x (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n))
                    (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ k))))
    (myMul (myMul x (Nat.succ m)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m (ratDenRep n k))))
    (congrArg.{1} (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul x Z) (denD_R m n k))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (myMul x (myMul (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n))
                      (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ k))))
      (myMul (myMul x (Nat.succ m))
             (myMul (Nat.succ m) (myMul (Nat.succ n) (Nat.succ k))))
      (myMul (myMul x (Nat.succ m)) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m (ratDenRep n k))))
      (den_rotate x (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n) (Nat.succ k))
      (congrArg.{1}
        (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul (myMul x (Nat.succ m)) Z)
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m (ratDenRep n k)))
          (myMul (Nat.succ m) (myMul (Nat.succ n) (Nat.succ k)))
          (denD_L m n k))))

theorem ratMul_add_aux (a b m c d n e f k : Nat) :

补充 · ratMul_add_pack 定理
theorem ratMul_add_pack (x y z : Prod (Prod Nat Nat) Nat) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep x (ratAddRep y z))
      (ratAddRep (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep x z)) :=
  match x with
  | prod_mk (prod_mk a b) m =>
    match y with
    | prod_mk (prod_mk c d) n =>
      match z with
      | prod_mk (prod_mk e f) k =>
        ratMul_add_aux a b m c d n e f k

III.7 · 本章第 7 / 11 单元 · 全书第 146 / 171 单元

146乘法的保序性：正因子不改变不等号

Unit 146 — Multiplication Preserves Order: Positive Factors Do Not Flip Inequalities

10 个定理 · 10 道练习 · 30 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

导数要回答的一个问题是：曲线整体乘上一个常数，斜率会怎么变？把逐点相乘写成差商，绕不开一条关于乘法的常识——正数乘在不等式两边，不等号的方向不变。这条常识在 ℚ 上不是免费的：乘法先通分再运算，正因子在分母里留下的因子要靠约分消掉，而约分本身要用乘法的交换、结合与分配。

练习 1ratLeRep_mul_pos_right定理附录解答

证明把 x ≤ y 与 0 < z 都拆到整数代表元。x ≤ y 是交叉相乘之后两个 ℕ 的和的大小比较；0 < z 说明 z 的分子两格一严一宽（f ≤ e），并且两格不相等，于是第二格可以写成第一格加上一个正数 t。把 e = f + t 代进目标，交叉乘积的两边都长出相同的四项，相消之后剩下的是 x ≤ y 两边同乘 t。ℕ 的乘法保序（leNat_mul_right）负责这一步，分配律负责把多出来的项摆回原位。

严格版本多一步：若乘完之后两边相等，就用非零因子消去律把 z 约掉，得到 x ~ y，与严格小矛盾。两个正数的积为正，是把 0 < y 乘上正数 x，得到 x·0 < x·y，再把左边收回零。

边界：只做正因子。负因子要把不等号翻过来，需要先接上取负与乘法的交换；常数倍的导数还要一条更细的估计，都留到后面的单元。不新增公理。

正因子乘在右边

变量写成展开后的序对类型，match 才能在证明里拆开三个代表元；结论里的 ratRep 只是它的定义别名，调用处照旧传有理数。

theorem ratLeRep_mul_pos_right (x y z : Prod (Prod Nat Nat) Nat) :
    ratLeRep x y → ratLtRep ratZeroRep z →
      ratLeRep (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep y z) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

依次拆开 x、y、z；x ≤ y 用 mulRep_den_form 化成两个 ℕ 和的大小比较；0 < z 给出 f ≤ e 与「两格不相等」，由此把 e 写成 f + t。两边交叉相乘、约掉公共因子 (k+1) 之后，剩下的正是 x ≤ y 两边同乘 t。

提示 2目标形态

ratLeRep x y → 0 < z → ratLeRep (x*z) (y*z)。

提示 3关键件

mulRep_den_form · ratDenRep_succ · leNat_mul_right · mul_add_right · leNat_mul_cancel。

查看解答 定理 · 12 行

任意代表元：保序、严格保序、乘积为正

theorem ratLeRep_mul_pos_right (x y z : Prod (Prod Nat Nat) Nat) :
    ratLeRep x y → ratLtRep ratZeroRep z →
      ratLeRep (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep y z) :=
  match x with
  | prod_mk (prod_mk a b) m =>
    match y with
    | prod_mk (prod_mk c d) n =>
      match z with
      | prod_mk (prod_mk e f) k =>
        fun (hxy : ratLeRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n))
            (hz : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (qrep e f k)) =>
          ratLeRep_mul_pos_lit a b m c d n e f k hxy hz
练习 2ratLeRep_mul_pos_left定理附录解答
theorem ratLeRep_mul_pos_left (x y z : ratRep) (hxy : ratLeRep x y)
    (hz : ratLtRep ratZeroRep z) :
    ratLeRep (ratMulRep z x) (ratMulRep z y) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

先把两个积交换成 z * x 与 z * y，再把右边那条保序定理搬过来（ratLeRep_congr_gen 沿 ratRel 搬运弱序）。

theorem ratLeRep_mul_pos_left (x y z : ratRep) (hxy : ratLeRep x y)
    (hz : ratLtRep ratZeroRep z) :
    ratLeRep (ratMulRep z x) (ratMulRep z y) :=
  ratLeRep_congr_gen
    (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep z x) (ratMulRep y z) (ratMulRep z y)
    (ratMul_comm x z) (ratMul_comm y z)
    (ratLeRep_mul_pos_right x y z hxy hz)
练习 3ratLtRep_mul_pos_right定理附录解答

严格版本

theorem ratLtRep_mul_pos_right (x y z : ratRep) (hxy : ratLtRep x y)
    (hz : ratLtRep ratZeroRep z) :
    ratLtRep (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep y z) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

弱序那一半直接搬。若乘完之后两边相等，把 z 移到前面来用 ratMul_cancel 约掉（z 不为零来自 0 < z），得到 x ~ y，与严格小矛盾。

提示 2目标形态

ratLtRep (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep y z)。

提示 3关键件

ratLtRep_le · ratLeRep_mul_pos_right · ratMul_cancel · ratMul_comm · ratRel_trans_gen。

查看解答 定理 · 16 行
theorem ratLtRep_mul_pos_right (x y z : ratRep) (hxy : ratLtRep x y)
    (hz : ratLtRep ratZeroRep z) :
    ratLtRep (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep y z) :=
  And.intro
    (ratLeRep_mul_pos_right x y z (ratLtRep_le x y hxy) hz)
    (fun (heq : ratRel (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep y z)) =>
      (And.right hxy)
        (ratMul_cancel z x y
          (fun (h0 : ratRel z ratZeroRep) =>
            (And.right hz) (ratRel_symm z ratZeroRep h0))
          (ratRel_trans_gen
            (ratMulRep z x) (ratMulRep y z) (ratMulRep z y)
            (ratRel_trans_gen
              (ratMulRep z x) (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep y z)
              (ratMul_comm z x) heq)
            (ratMul_comm y z))))
练习 4ratLtRep_mul_pos_left定理附录解答
theorem ratLtRep_mul_pos_left (x y z : ratRep) (hxy : ratLtRep x y)
    (hz : ratLtRep ratZeroRep z) :
    ratLtRep (ratMulRep z x) (ratMulRep z y) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

与左边版本同构：先把乘积交换到右边，用完严格保序再沿 ratRel 把两个端点换回来。

提示 2目标形态

ratLtRep (ratMulRep z x) (ratMulRep z y)。

提示 3关键件

ratLtRep_congr_fst · ratLtRep_congr_snd · ratMul_comm · ratLtRep_mul_pos_right。

查看解答 定理 · 9 行
theorem ratLtRep_mul_pos_left (x y z : ratRep) (hxy : ratLtRep x y)
    (hz : ratLtRep ratZeroRep z) :
    ratLtRep (ratMulRep z x) (ratMulRep z y) :=
  ratLtRep_congr_fst
    (ratMulRep z x) (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep z y)
    (ratLtRep_congr_snd (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep y z) (ratMulRep z y)
      (ratLtRep_mul_pos_right x y z hxy hz)
      (ratMul_comm y z))
    (ratRel_symm (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep z x) (ratMul_comm x z))

乘法的保序性：正因子不改变不等号

10 道练习 · 10 份按名匹配的解答 · 44 个解答独有声明

练习 1 · ratLeRep_mul_pos_right 定理

任意代表元：保序、严格保序、乘积为正

theorem ratLeRep_mul_pos_right (x y z : Prod (Prod Nat Nat) Nat) :
    ratLeRep x y → ratLtRep ratZeroRep z →
      ratLeRep (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep y z) :=
  match x with
  | prod_mk (prod_mk a b) m =>
    match y with
    | prod_mk (prod_mk c d) n =>
      match z with
      | prod_mk (prod_mk e f) k =>
        fun (hxy : ratLeRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n))
            (hz : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (qrep e f k)) =>
          ratLeRep_mul_pos_lit a b m c d n e f k hxy hz
练习 2 · ratLeRep_mul_pos_left 定理
theorem ratLeRep_mul_pos_left (x y z : ratRep) (hxy : ratLeRep x y)
    (hz : ratLtRep ratZeroRep z) :
    ratLeRep (ratMulRep z x) (ratMulRep z y) :=
  ratLeRep_congr_gen
    (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep z x) (ratMulRep y z) (ratMulRep z y)
    (ratMul_comm x z) (ratMul_comm y z)
    (ratLeRep_mul_pos_right x y z hxy hz)
练习 3 · ratLtRep_mul_pos_right 定理
theorem ratLtRep_mul_pos_right (x y z : ratRep) (hxy : ratLtRep x y)
    (hz : ratLtRep ratZeroRep z) :
    ratLtRep (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep y z) :=
  And.intro
    (ratLeRep_mul_pos_right x y z (ratLtRep_le x y hxy) hz)
    (fun (heq : ratRel (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep y z)) =>
      (And.right hxy)
        (ratMul_cancel z x y
          (fun (h0 : ratRel z ratZeroRep) =>
            (And.right hz) (ratRel_symm z ratZeroRep h0))
          (ratRel_trans_gen
            (ratMulRep z x) (ratMulRep y z) (ratMulRep z y)
            (ratRel_trans_gen
              (ratMulRep z x) (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep y z)
              (ratMul_comm z x) heq)
            (ratMul_comm y z))))
练习 4 · ratLtRep_mul_pos_left 定理
theorem ratLtRep_mul_pos_left (x y z : ratRep) (hxy : ratLtRep x y)
    (hz : ratLtRep ratZeroRep z) :
    ratLtRep (ratMulRep z x) (ratMulRep z y) :=
  ratLtRep_congr_fst
    (ratMulRep z x) (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep z y)
    (ratLtRep_congr_snd (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep y z) (ratMulRep z y)
      (ratLtRep_mul_pos_right x y z hxy hz)
      (ratMul_comm y z))
    (ratRel_symm (ratMulRep x z) (ratMulRep z x) (ratMul_comm x z))
练习 5 · ratMul_pos 定理
theorem ratMul_pos (x y : ratRep) (hx : ratLtRep ratZeroRep x)
    (hy : ratLtRep ratZeroRep y) :
    ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratMulRep x y) :=
  ratLtRep_congr_fst ratZeroRep (ratMulRep x ratZeroRep) (ratMulRep x y)
    (ratLtRep_mul_pos_left ratZeroRep y x hy hx)
    (ratRel_symm (ratMulRep x ratZeroRep) ratZeroRep (ratMul_zero_right x))
练习 6 · ratMul_pos_self 定理
theorem ratMul_pos_self (x : ratRep) (hx : ratLtRep ratZeroRep x) :
    ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratMulRep x x) :=
  ratMul_pos x x hx hx

补充 · leNat_add_left 定理
theorem leNat_add_left (a b k : Nat) (h : LeNat a b) :
    LeNat (Nat.add k a) (Nat.add k b) :=
  @Eq.subst.{1} Nat (fun (Z : Nat) => LeNat Z (Nat.add k b))
    (Nat.add a k) (Nat.add k a)
    (add_comm a k)
    (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat (fun (Z : Nat) => LeNat (Nat.add a k) Z)
      (Nat.add b k) (Nat.add k b)
      (add_comm b k)
      (leNat_add_right a b k h))
补充 · p_expand 定理
theorem p_expand (a b f t : Nat) :
    Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.add f t)) (myMul b f) =
      Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b f)) (myMul a t) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.add f t)) (myMul b f))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul a t)) (myMul b f))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b f)) (myMul a t))
    (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => Nat.add Z (myMul b f)) (mul_add a f t))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul a t)) (myMul b f))
      (Nat.add (myMul a f) (Nat.add (myMul a t) (myMul b f)))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b f)) (myMul a t))
      (add_assoc (myMul a f) (myMul a t) (myMul b f))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a f) (Nat.add (myMul a t) (myMul b f)))
        (Nat.add (myMul a f) (Nat.add (myMul b f) (myMul a t)))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b f)) (myMul a t))
        (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => Nat.add (myMul a f) Z)
          (add_comm (myMul a t) (myMul b f)))
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b f)) (myMul a t))
          (Nat.add (myMul a f) (Nat.add (myMul b f) (myMul a t)))
          (add_assoc (myMul a f) (myMul b f) (myMul a t)))))

补充 · mul_rotate_den 定理
theorem mul_rotate_den (a t n : Nat) :
    myMul (myMul a t) (Nat.succ n) = myMul (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) t :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (myMul (myMul a t) (Nat.succ n))
    (myMul a (myMul t (Nat.succ n)))
    (myMul (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) t)
    (myMul_assoc a t (Nat.succ n))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (myMul a (myMul t (Nat.succ n)))
      (myMul a (myMul (Nat.succ n) t))
      (myMul (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) t)
      (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul a Z) (myMul_comm t (Nat.succ n)))
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
        (myMul (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) t)
        (myMul a (myMul (Nat.succ n) t))
        (myMul_assoc a (Nat.succ n) t)))
补充 · p_mul 定理
theorem p_mul (a b f t n : Nat) :
    P a b f t n = Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (myMul (Ax a n) t) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.add f t)) (myMul b f)) (Nat.succ n))
    (myMul (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b f)) (myMul a t))
      (Nat.succ n))
    (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (myMul (Ax a n) t))
    (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul Z (Nat.succ n)) (p_expand a b f t))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (myMul (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b f)) (myMul a t))
        (Nat.succ n))
      (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (myMul (myMul a t) (Nat.succ n)))
      (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (myMul (Ax a n) t))
      (mul_add_right (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b f)) (myMul a t)
        (Nat.succ n))
      (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => Nat.add (A1 a b f n) Z)
        (mul_rotate_den a t n)))

      (myMul (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul c f) (myMul d f)) (myMul d t))

        (mul_rotate_den d t m)))
