      (mul_add_right (Nat.add (myMul c f) (myMul d f)) (myMul c t)
        (Nat.succ m))
      (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => Nat.add (A2 c d f m) Z)
        (mul_rotate_den c t m)))
补充 · s_mul 定理
theorem s_mul (a b f t n : Nat) :
    S a b f t n = Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (myMul (By b n) t) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b (Nat.add f t))) (Nat.succ n))
    (myMul (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b f)) (myMul b t))
      (Nat.succ n))
    (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (myMul (By b n) t))
    (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul Z (Nat.succ n)) (s_expand a b f t))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (myMul (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b f)) (myMul b t))
        (Nat.succ n))
      (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (myMul (myMul b t) (Nat.succ n)))
      (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (myMul (By b n) t))
      (mul_add_right (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b f)) (myMul b t)
        (Nat.succ n))
      (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => Nat.add (A1 a b f n) Z)
        (mul_rotate_den b t n)))
补充 · sum_pack 定理
theorem sum_pack (B1 x B2 y t : Nat) :
    Nat.add (Nat.add B1 (myMul x t)) (Nat.add B2 (myMul y t)) =
      Nat.add (Nat.add B1 B2) (myMul (Nat.add x y) t) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add (Nat.add B1 (myMul x t)) (Nat.add B2 (myMul y t)))
    (Nat.add B1 (Nat.add (myMul x t) (Nat.add B2 (myMul y t))))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add B1 B2) (myMul (Nat.add x y) t))
    (add_assoc B1 (myMul x t) (Nat.add B2 (myMul y t)))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add B1 (Nat.add (myMul x t) (Nat.add B2 (myMul y t))))
      (Nat.add B1 (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul x t) B2) (myMul y t)))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add B1 B2) (myMul (Nat.add x y) t))
      (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => Nat.add B1 Z)
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul x t) B2) (myMul y t))
          (Nat.add (myMul x t) (Nat.add B2 (myMul y t)))
          (add_assoc (myMul x t) B2 (myMul y t))))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add B1 (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul x t) B2) (myMul y t)))
        (Nat.add B1 (Nat.add (Nat.add B2 (myMul x t)) (myMul y t)))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add B1 B2) (myMul (Nat.add x y) t))
        (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => Nat.add B1 Z)
          (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => Nat.add Z (myMul y t))
            (add_comm (myMul x t) B2)))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add B1 (Nat.add (Nat.add B2 (myMul x t)) (myMul y t)))
          (Nat.add B1 (Nat.add B2 (Nat.add (myMul x t) (myMul y t))))
          (Nat.add (Nat.add B1 B2) (myMul (Nat.add x y) t))
          (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => Nat.add B1 Z)
            (add_assoc B2 (myMul x t) (myMul y t)))
          (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
            (Nat.add B1 (Nat.add B2 (Nat.add (myMul x t) (myMul y t))))
            (Nat.add (Nat.add B1 B2) (Nat.add (myMul x t) (myMul y t)))
            (Nat.add (Nat.add B1 B2) (myMul (Nat.add x y) t))
            (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
              (Nat.add (Nat.add B1 B2) (Nat.add (myMul x t) (myMul y t)))
              (Nat.add B1 (Nat.add B2 (Nat.add (myMul x t) (myMul y t))))
              (add_assoc B1 B2 (Nat.add (myMul x t) (myMul y t))))
            (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => Nat.add (Nat.add B1 B2) Z)
              (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
                (myMul (Nat.add x y) t)
                (Nat.add (myMul x t) (myMul y t))
                (mul_add_right x y t)))))))
补充 · left_pack 定理
theorem left_pack (a b m c d n f t : Nat) :
    Nat.add (P a b f t n) (Q c d f t m) =
      Nat.add (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (A2 c d f m))
        (myMul (Nat.add (Ax a n) (Dx d m)) t) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add (P a b f t n) (Q c d f t m))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (myMul (Ax a n) t)) (Q c d f t m))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (A2 c d f m))
      (myMul (Nat.add (Ax a n) (Dx d m)) t))
    (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => Nat.add Z (Q c d f t m)) (p_mul a b f t n))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (myMul (Ax a n) t)) (Q c d f t m))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (myMul (Ax a n) t))
        (Nat.add (A2 c d f m) (myMul (Dx d m) t)))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (A2 c d f m))
        (myMul (Nat.add (Ax a n) (Dx d m)) t))
      (congrArg
        (fun (Z : Nat) =>
          Nat.add (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (myMul (Ax a n) t)) Z)
        (q_mul c d f t m))
      (sum_pack (A1 a b f n) (Ax a n) (A2 c d f m) (Dx d m) t))
补充 · right_pack 定理
theorem right_pack (a b m c d n f t : Nat) :
    Nat.add (R c d f t m) (S a b f t n) =
      Nat.add (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (A2 c d f m))
        (myMul (Nat.add (Cx c m) (By b n)) t) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add (R c d f t m) (S a b f t n))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add (A2 c d f m) (myMul (Cx c m) t)) (S a b f t n))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (A2 c d f m))
      (myMul (Nat.add (Cx c m) (By b n)) t))
    (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => Nat.add Z (S a b f t n)) (r_mul c d f t m))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (A2 c d f m) (myMul (Cx c m) t)) (S a b f t n))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (A2 c d f m) (myMul (Cx c m) t))
        (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (myMul (By b n) t)))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (A2 c d f m))
        (myMul (Nat.add (Cx c m) (By b n)) t))
      (congrArg
        (fun (Z : Nat) =>
          Nat.add (Nat.add (A2 c d f m) (myMul (Cx c m) t)) Z)
        (s_mul a b f t n))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (Nat.add (A2 c d f m) (myMul (Cx c m) t))
          (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (myMul (By b n) t)))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add (A2 c d f m) (A1 a b f n))
          (myMul (Nat.add (Cx c m) (By b n)) t))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (A2 c d f m))
          (myMul (Nat.add (Cx c m) (By b n)) t))
        (sum_pack (A2 c d f m) (Cx c m) (A1 a b f n) (By b n) t)
        (congrArg
          (fun (Z : Nat) =>
            Nat.add Z (myMul (Nat.add (Cx c m) (By b n)) t))
          (add_comm (A2 c d f m) (A1 a b f n)))))
补充 · mul_pos_core 定理
theorem mul_pos_core (a b m c d n f t : Nat)
    (h : LeNat (Nat.add (Ax a n) (Dx d m))
               (Nat.add (Cx c m) (By b n))) :
    LeNat (Nat.add (P a b f t n) (Q c d f t m))
          (Nat.add (R c d f t m) (S a b f t n)) :=
  @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
    (fun (Z : Nat) => LeNat Z (Nat.add (R c d f t m) (S a b f t n)))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (A2 c d f m))
      (myMul (Nat.add (Ax a n) (Dx d m)) t))
    (Nat.add (P a b f t n) (Q c d f t m))
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (P a b f t n) (Q c d f t m))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (A2 c d f m))
        (myMul (Nat.add (Ax a n) (Dx d m)) t))
      (left_pack a b m c d n f t))
    (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
      (fun (Z : Nat) =>
        LeNat
          (Nat.add (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (A2 c d f m))
            (myMul (Nat.add (Ax a n) (Dx d m)) t)) Z)
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (A2 c d f m))
        (myMul (Nat.add (Cx c m) (By b n)) t))
      (Nat.add (R c d f t m) (S a b f t n))
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (R c d f t m) (S a b f t n))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (A2 c d f m))
          (myMul (Nat.add (Cx c m) (By b n)) t))
        (right_pack a b m c d n f t))
      (leNat_add_left
        (myMul (Nat.add (Ax a n) (Dx d m)) t)
        (myMul (Nat.add (Cx c m) (By b n)) t)
        (Nat.add (A1 a b f n) (A2 c d f m))
        (leNat_mul_right (Nat.add (Ax a n) (Dx d m))
          (Nat.add (Cx c m) (By b n)) t h)))
补充 · ratLeRep_lit 定理
theorem ratLeRep_lit (a b m c d n : Nat) :
    ratLeRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n) ↔
      LeNat (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n))) :=
  Iff.intro
    (fun (h : ratLeRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n)) =>
      leRep_wd_pair
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den n))
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n)))
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den m))
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
        (intRel_of_eq (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den n))
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n)))
          (mulRep_den_form (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) n))
        (intRel_of_eq (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den m))
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
          (mulRep_den_form (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) m))
        h)
    (fun (h : LeNat
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n)))) =>
      leRep_wd_pair
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n)))
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den n))
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den m))
        (intRel_of_eq
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n)))
          (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den n))
          (@Eq.symm.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
            (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den n))
            (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n)))
            (mulRep_den_form (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) n)))
        (intRel_of_eq
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
          (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den m))
          (@Eq.symm.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
            (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den m))
            (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
            (mulRep_den_form (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) m)))
        h)
补充 · ratLtRep_zero_lit 定理
theorem ratLtRep_zero_lit (e f k : Nat)
    (h : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (qrep e f k)) : LeNat f e :=
  @Eq.subst.{1} Nat (fun (Z : Nat) => LeNat f Z)
    (Nat.add e Nat.zero) e
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat e (Nat.add e Nat.zero) (add_zero e))
    (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat (fun (Z : Nat) => LeNat Z (Nat.add e Nat.zero))
      (Nat.add Nat.zero f) f (zero_add f)
      (@Eq.subst.{1} Prop (fun (P : Prop) => P)
        (leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f))
        (LeNat (Nat.add Nat.zero f) (Nat.add e Nat.zero))
        (leRep_mk Nat.zero Nat.zero e f)
        (leRep_wd_pair
          (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero) (den k))
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero)
          (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den Nat.zero))
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)
          (intRel_of_eq
            (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero) (den k))
            (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero)
            (@Eq.trans.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
              (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero) (den k))
              (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul Nat.zero (Nat.succ k))
                (myMul Nat.zero (Nat.succ k)))
              (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero)
              (mulRep_den_form (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero) k)
              (prod_mk_congr
                (myMul Nat.zero (Nat.succ k)) Nat.zero
                (myMul Nat.zero (Nat.succ k)) Nat.zero
                (zero_mul (Nat.succ k)) (zero_mul (Nat.succ k)))))
          (intRel_of_eq
            (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den Nat.zero))
            (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)
            (mulRep_den_form (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) Nat.zero))
          (And.left h))))
补充 · ratRel_zero_lit 定理
theorem ratRel_zero_lit (e f k : Nat) :
    ratRel ratZeroRep (qrep e f k) ↔ e = f :=
  Iff.intro
    (fun (h : ratRel ratZeroRep (qrep e f k)) =>
      @Iff.mp (intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero))
        (e = f)
        (intRel_zero_iff e f)
        (@Iff.mp (ratRel (qrep e f k) ratZeroRep)
          (intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero))
          (ratRel_zero_iff_num_zero e f k)
          (ratRel_symm ratZeroRep (qrep e f k) h)))
    (fun (h : e = f) =>
      ratRel_symm (qrep e f k) ratZeroRep
        (@Iff.mpr (ratRel (qrep e f k) ratZeroRep)
          (intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero))
          (ratRel_zero_iff_num_zero e f k)
          (@Iff.mpr (intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (@prod_mk Nat Nat Nat.zero Nat.zero))
            (e = f)
            (intRel_zero_iff e f) h)))
补充 · NumX 定义
def NumX (a b e f : Nat) : Nat := Nat.add (myMul a e) (myMul b f)
补充 · NumX2 定义
def NumX2 (a b e f : Nat) : Nat := Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b e)
补充 · NumY 定义
def NumY (c d e f : Nat) : Nat := Nat.add (myMul c e) (myMul d f)
补充 · NumY2 定义
def NumY2 (c d e f : Nat) : Nat := Nat.add (myMul c f) (myMul d e)
补充 · DenN 定义
def DenN (n k : Nat) : Nat := myMul (Nat.succ n) (Nat.succ k)
补充 · LX 定义
def LX (a b m c d n e f k : Nat) : Nat :=
  Nat.add (myMul (NumX a b e f) (DenN n k))
          (myMul (NumY2 c d e f) (DenN m k))
补充 · RX 定义
def RX (a b m c d n e f k : Nat) : Nat :=
  Nat.add (myMul (NumY c d e f) (DenN m k))
          (myMul (NumX2 a b e f) (DenN n k))
补充 · ratLeRep_mul_lit 定理
theorem ratLeRep_mul_lit (a b m c d n e f k : Nat) :
    ratLeRep (ratMulRep (qrep a b m) (qrep e f k))
             (ratMulRep (qrep c d n) (qrep e f k)) ↔
      LeNat (LX a b m c d n e f k) (RX a b m c d n e f k) :=
  Iff.intro
    (fun (h : ratLeRep (ratMulRep (qrep a b m) (qrep e f k))
                       (ratMulRep (qrep c d n) (qrep e f k))) =>
      leRep_wd_pair
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumX a b e f) (NumX2 a b e f))
          (den (ratDenRep n k)))
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (NumX a b e f) (DenN n k))
          (myMul (NumX2 a b e f) (DenN n k)))
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumY c d e f) (NumY2 c d e f))
          (den (ratDenRep m k)))
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (NumY c d e f) (DenN m k))
          (myMul (NumY2 c d e f) (DenN m k)))
        (intRel_of_eq
          (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumX a b e f) (NumX2 a b e f))
            (den (ratDenRep n k)))
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (NumX a b e f) (DenN n k))
            (myMul (NumX2 a b e f) (DenN n k)))
          (@Eq.trans.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
            (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumX a b e f) (NumX2 a b e f))
              (den (ratDenRep n k)))
            (@prod_mk Nat Nat
              (myMul (NumX a b e f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep n k)))
              (myMul (NumX2 a b e f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep n k))))
            (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (NumX a b e f) (DenN n k))
              (myMul (NumX2 a b e f) (DenN n k)))
            (mulRep_den_form
              (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumX a b e f) (NumX2 a b e f))
              (ratDenRep n k))
            (prod_mk_congr
              (myMul (NumX a b e f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep n k)))
              (myMul (NumX a b e f) (DenN n k))
              (myMul (NumX2 a b e f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep n k)))
              (myMul (NumX2 a b e f) (DenN n k))
              (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul (NumX a b e f) Z)
                (ratDenRep_succ n k))
              (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul (NumX2 a b e f) Z)
                (ratDenRep_succ n k)))))
        (intRel_of_eq
          (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumY c d e f) (NumY2 c d e f))
            (den (ratDenRep m k)))
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (NumY c d e f) (DenN m k))
            (myMul (NumY2 c d e f) (DenN m k)))
          (@Eq.trans.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
            (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumY c d e f) (NumY2 c d e f))
              (den (ratDenRep m k)))
            (@prod_mk Nat Nat
              (myMul (NumY c d e f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
              (myMul (NumY2 c d e f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k))))
            (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (NumY c d e f) (DenN m k))
              (myMul (NumY2 c d e f) (DenN m k)))
            (mulRep_den_form
              (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumY c d e f) (NumY2 c d e f))
              (ratDenRep m k))
            (prod_mk_congr
              (myMul (NumY c d e f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
              (myMul (NumY c d e f) (DenN m k))
              (myMul (NumY2 c d e f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
              (myMul (NumY2 c d e f) (DenN m k))
              (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul (NumY c d e f) Z)
                (ratDenRep_succ m k))
              (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul (NumY2 c d e f) Z)
                (ratDenRep_succ m k)))))
        h)
    (fun (h : LeNat (LX a b m c d n e f k) (RX a b m c d n e f k)) =>
      leRep_wd_pair
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (NumX a b e f) (DenN n k))
          (myMul (NumX2 a b e f) (DenN n k)))
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumX a b e f) (NumX2 a b e f))
          (den (ratDenRep n k)))
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (NumY c d e f) (DenN m k))
          (myMul (NumY2 c d e f) (DenN m k)))
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumY c d e f) (NumY2 c d e f))
          (den (ratDenRep m k)))
        (intRel_of_eq
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (NumX a b e f) (DenN n k))
            (myMul (NumX2 a b e f) (DenN n k)))
          (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumX a b e f) (NumX2 a b e f))
            (den (ratDenRep n k)))
          (@Eq.symm.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
            (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumX a b e f) (NumX2 a b e f))
              (den (ratDenRep n k)))
            (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (NumX a b e f) (DenN n k))
              (myMul (NumX2 a b e f) (DenN n k)))
            (@Eq.trans.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
              (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumX a b e f) (NumX2 a b e f))
                (den (ratDenRep n k)))
              (@prod_mk Nat Nat
                (myMul (NumX a b e f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep n k)))
                (myMul (NumX2 a b e f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep n k))))
              (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (NumX a b e f) (DenN n k))
                (myMul (NumX2 a b e f) (DenN n k)))
              (mulRep_den_form
                (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumX a b e f) (NumX2 a b e f))
                (ratDenRep n k))
              (prod_mk_congr
                (myMul (NumX a b e f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep n k)))
                (myMul (NumX a b e f) (DenN n k))
                (myMul (NumX2 a b e f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep n k)))
                (myMul (NumX2 a b e f) (DenN n k))
                (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul (NumX a b e f) Z)
                  (ratDenRep_succ n k))
                (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul (NumX2 a b e f) Z)
                  (ratDenRep_succ n k))))))
        (intRel_of_eq
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (NumY c d e f) (DenN m k))
            (myMul (NumY2 c d e f) (DenN m k)))
          (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumY c d e f) (NumY2 c d e f))
            (den (ratDenRep m k)))
          (@Eq.symm.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
            (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumY c d e f) (NumY2 c d e f))
              (den (ratDenRep m k)))
            (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (NumY c d e f) (DenN m k))
              (myMul (NumY2 c d e f) (DenN m k)))
            (@Eq.trans.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
              (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumY c d e f) (NumY2 c d e f))
                (den (ratDenRep m k)))
              (@prod_mk Nat Nat
                (myMul (NumY c d e f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                (myMul (NumY2 c d e f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k))))
              (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (NumY c d e f) (DenN m k))
                (myMul (NumY2 c d e f) (DenN m k)))
              (mulRep_den_form
                (@prod_mk Nat Nat (NumY c d e f) (NumY2 c d e f))
                (ratDenRep m k))
              (prod_mk_congr
                (myMul (NumY c d e f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                (myMul (NumY c d e f) (DenN m k))
                (myMul (NumY2 c d e f) (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m k)))
                (myMul (NumY2 c d e f) (DenN m k))
                (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul (NumY c d e f) Z)
                  (ratDenRep_succ m k))
                (congrArg (fun (Z : Nat) => myMul (NumY2 c d e f) Z)
                  (ratDenRep_succ m k))))))
        h)
补充 · leNat_congr 定理
theorem leNat_congr (a b a' b' : Nat) (h1 : a = a') (h2 : b = b')
    (h : LeNat a b) : LeNat a' b' :=
  @Eq.subst.{1} Nat (fun (Z : Nat) => LeNat a' Z) b b' h2
    (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat (fun (Z : Nat) => LeNat Z b) a a' h1 h)

theorem mul_pos_pack (a b m c d n f t k : Nat)
    (h : LeNat (Nat.add (Ax a n) (Dx d m))
               (Nat.add (Cx c m) (By b n))) :
    LeNat
      (Nat.add (myMul (P a b f t n) (Nat.succ k))
               (myMul (Q c d f t m) (Nat.succ k)))
      (Nat.add (myMul (R c d f t m) (Nat.succ k))
               (myMul (S a b f t n) (Nat.succ k))) :=

    (myMul (Nat.add (P a b f t n) (Q c d f t m)) (Nat.succ k))
    (myMul (Nat.add (R c d f t m) (S a b f t n)) (Nat.succ k))

             (myMul (Q c d f t m) (Nat.succ k)))
    (Nat.add (myMul (R c d f t m) (Nat.succ k))
             (myMul (S a b f t n) (Nat.succ k)))
    (mul_add_right (P a b f t n) (Q c d f t m) (Nat.succ k))
    (mul_add_right (R c d f t m) (S a b f t n) (Nat.succ k))

      (mul_pos_core a b m c d n f t h))

theorem assoc_first (a b m c d n f t k : Nat) :
    Nat.add
      (myMul (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.add f t)) (myMul b f)) (Nat.succ n))
        (Nat.succ k))

补充 · mul_pos_assoc 定理
theorem mul_pos_assoc (a b m c d n f t k : Nat)
    (h : LeNat
      (Nat.add (myMul (P a b f t n) (Nat.succ k))
               (myMul (Q c d f t m) (Nat.succ k)))
      (Nat.add (myMul (R c d f t m) (Nat.succ k))
               (myMul (S a b f t n) (Nat.succ k)))) :
    LeNat (LX a b m c d n (Nat.add f t) f k)
          (RX a b m c d n (Nat.add f t) f k) :=
  leNat_congr
    (Nat.add (myMul (P a b f t n) (Nat.succ k))
             (myMul (Q c d f t m) (Nat.succ k)))
    (Nat.add (myMul (R c d f t m) (Nat.succ k))
             (myMul (S a b f t n) (Nat.succ k)))
    (Nat.add
      (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.add f t)) (myMul b f)) (DenN n k))
      (myMul (Nat.add (myMul c f) (myMul d (Nat.add f t))) (DenN m k)))
    (Nat.add
      (myMul (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.add f t)) (myMul d f)) (DenN m k))
      (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b (Nat.add f t))) (DenN n k)))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul (P a b f t n) (Nat.succ k))
               (myMul (Q c d f t m) (Nat.succ k)))
      (Nat.add
        (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.add f t)) (myMul b f)) (DenN n k))
        (myMul (Q c d f t m) (Nat.succ k)))
      (Nat.add
        (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.add f t)) (myMul b f)) (DenN n k))
        (myMul (Nat.add (myMul c f) (myMul d (Nat.add f t))) (DenN m k)))
      (assoc_first a b m c d n f t k)
      (assoc_second a b m c d n f t k))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul (R c d f t m) (Nat.succ k))
               (myMul (S a b f t n) (Nat.succ k)))
      (Nat.add
        (myMul (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.add f t)) (myMul d f)) (DenN m k))
        (myMul (S a b f t n) (Nat.succ k)))
      (Nat.add
        (myMul (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.add f t)) (myMul d f)) (DenN m k))
        (myMul (Nat.add (myMul a f) (myMul b (Nat.add f t))) (DenN n k)))
      (assoc_third a b m c d n f t k)
      (assoc_fourth a b m c d n f t k))
    h
补充 · e_split 定理
theorem e_split (e f : Nat) (hfe : LeNat f e) (hne : ¬ e = f) :
    ∃ (t : Nat), e = Nat.add f t :=
  @Or.elim (f = e) (LeNat (Nat.succ f) e)
    (∃ (t : Nat), e = Nat.add f t)
    (fun (heq : f = e) =>
      @False.elim (∃ (t : Nat), e = Nat.add f t)
        (hne (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat f e heq)))
    (fun (hlt : LeNat (Nat.succ f) e) =>
      Exists.elim (leNat_exists_add (Nat.succ f) e hlt)
        (fun (r : Nat) (hr : e = Nat.add (Nat.succ f) r) =>
          Exists.intro (Nat.succ r)
            (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat e (Nat.add (Nat.succ f) r)
              (Nat.add f (Nat.succ r)) hr
              (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
                (Nat.add (Nat.succ f) r)
                (Nat.succ (Nat.add f r))
                (Nat.add f (Nat.succ r))
                (succ_add f r)
                (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add f (Nat.succ r))
                  (Nat.succ (Nat.add f r)) (add_succ f r))))))
    (leNat_eq_or_succ f e hfe)
补充 · ratLeRep_mul_pos_lit 定理
theorem ratLeRep_mul_pos_lit (a b m c d n e f k : Nat)
    (hxy : ratLeRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n))
    (hz : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (qrep e f k)) :
    ratLeRep (ratMulRep (qrep a b m) (qrep e f k))
             (ratMulRep (qrep c d n) (qrep e f k)) :=
  Iff.mpr (ratLeRep_mul_lit a b m c d n e f k)
    (@Exists.elim Nat (fun (t : Nat) => e = Nat.add f t)
      (LeNat (LX a b m c d n e f k) (RX a b m c d n e f k))
      (e_split e f (ratLtRep_zero_lit e f k hz)
        (fun (heq : e = f) =>
          (And.right hz)
            (@Iff.mpr (ratRel ratZeroRep (qrep e f k)) (e = f)
              (ratRel_zero_lit e f k) heq)))
      (fun (t : Nat) (ht : e = Nat.add f t) =>
        @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
          (fun (Z : Nat) =>
            LeNat (LX a b m c d n Z f k) (RX a b m c d n Z f k))
          (Nat.add f t) e
          (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat e (Nat.add f t) ht)
          (mul_pos_assoc a b m c d n f t k
            (mul_pos_pack a b m c d n f t k
              (@Iff.mp (ratLeRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n))
                (LeNat (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
                       (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n))))
                (ratLeRep_lit a b m c d n) hxy)))))

III.7 · 本章第 8 / 11 单元 · 全书第 147 / 171 单元

147乘法的符号：负因子把不等号翻过来

Unit 147 — Signs of Products: Negative Factors Flip Inequalities

9 个定理 · 9 道练习 · 27 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

曲线在一点取极大值，意味着往左往右走，函数值都不再增加。把这句话翻译成斜率，最后要判断两个量相乘的符号：自变量的改变是正还是负，函数值的改变又跟着谁走。正正得正、正负得负、负负得正，这条关于乘法的常识在 ℚ 上要回到代表元才能落地。

练习 1ratLtRep_of_neg_pos定理附录解答

证明的骨架是单元141 的 ratMul_neg：x·(-y) ~ -(x·y)。把 (-x)·y 先交换成 y·(-x)，再用这条等式收成 -(x·y)；于是「负数乘正数」化归为「正数乘正数的积取负」。单元146 的 ratMul_pos 给出正积，ratLtRep_neg_of_pos 与 ratLtRep_pos_of_neg 负责把积的符号翻回去。两个负因子相乘则连翻两次，回到正积。

边界：只做符号的乘法表（同号得正、异号得负），不反过来从积的符号反推因子的符号；那一步要用「积为零则因子为零」，留到后面的单元。函数仍取 ratRep → ratRep，不新增公理，也不碰 kernel/front。

取负把严格序翻回去

theorem ratLtRep_of_neg_pos (z : ratRep)
    (h : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratNegRep z)) :
    ratLtRep z ratZeroRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把 ratLtRep_neg_of_pos 用在 -z 上，得到 -(-z) < 0；再把左端点沿 -(-z) ~ z 换回来。

提示 2目标形态

ratLtRep z ratZeroRep。

提示 3关键件

ratLtRep_neg_of_pos · ratNegRep_invol · ratLtRep_congr_fst。

查看解答 定理 · 6 行

曲线在一点取极大值，意味着往左往右走函数值都不增加。把这件事翻译成 斜率，就要知道两个差的符号：自变量往右挪是正的还是负的，函数值的改变 又跟着谁走。导数的判别最后落在一句常识上——正正得正、正负得负、 负负得正。这条常识在 ℚ 上要回到代表元，把取负与乘法的交换接起来。 证明的骨架是单元141 的 ratMul_neg：x·(-y) ~ -(x·y)。把 (-x)·y 先交换成 y·(-x)，再用这条等式收成 -(x·y)；于是「负数乘正数」化归 为「正数乘正数的积取负」。单元146 的 ratMul_pos 给出正积， ratLtRep_neg_of_pos 与 ratLtRep_pos_of_neg 负责把积的符号翻回去。 0 < -z ⇒ z < 0：把 ratLtRep_neg_of_pos 用在 -z 上，得到 -(-z) < 0，再把左端点沿 -(-z) ~ z 换回来。

theorem ratLtRep_of_neg_pos (z : ratRep)
    (h : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratNegRep z)) :
    ratLtRep z ratZeroRep :=
  ratLtRep_congr_fst z (ratNegRep (ratNegRep z)) ratZeroRep
    (ratLtRep_neg_of_pos (ratNegRep z) h)
    (ratRel_symm z (ratNegRep (ratNegRep z)) (ratNegRep_invol z))
练习 2ratLtRep_of_neg_neg定理附录解答
theorem ratLtRep_of_neg_neg (z : ratRep)
    (h : ratLtRep (ratNegRep z) ratZeroRep) :
    ratLtRep ratZeroRep z :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

同样先翻一次，得到 0 < -(-z)；再把右端点沿 -(-z) ~ z 换回 z。

提示 2目标形态

ratLtRep ratZeroRep z。

提示 3关键件

ratLtRep_pos_of_neg · ratNegRep_invol · ratLtRep_congr_snd。

查看解答 定理 · 6 行

反方向：-z < 0 ⇒ 0 < z。同样先翻一次，再把右端点换回 z。

theorem ratLtRep_of_neg_neg (z : ratRep)
    (h : ratLtRep (ratNegRep z) ratZeroRep) :
    ratLtRep ratZeroRep z :=
  ratLtRep_congr_snd ratZeroRep (ratNegRep (ratNegRep z)) z
    (ratLtRep_pos_of_neg (ratNegRep z) h)
    (ratNegRep_invol z)
练习 3ratMul_neg_left定理附录解答

负因子乘在左边

theorem ratMul_neg_left (x y : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep (ratNegRep x) y) (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x y)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

先把 (-x)·y 交换成 y·(-x)，用 ratMul_neg 收成 -(y·x)，再把里面的 y·x 换成 x·y。

提示 2目标形态

ratRel (ratMulRep (ratNegRep x) y) (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x y))。

提示 3关键件

ratMul_comm · ratMul_neg · ratNegRep_wd · ratRel_trans_gen。

查看解答 定理 · 14 行

(-x)·y ~ -(x·y)：交换之后就是 y·(-x)，交给单元141 的 ratMul_neg， 最后把里面那个 y·x 换成 x·y。

theorem ratMul_neg_left (x y : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep (ratNegRep x) y) (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x y)) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (ratMulRep (ratNegRep x) y)
    (ratNegRep (ratMulRep y x))
    (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x y))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratMulRep (ratNegRep x) y)
      (ratMulRep y (ratNegRep x))
      (ratNegRep (ratMulRep y x))
      (ratMul_comm (ratNegRep x) y)
      (ratMul_neg y x))
    (ratNegRep_wd (ratMulRep y x) (ratMulRep x y)
      (ratRel_symm (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep y x) (ratMul_comm x y)))
练习 4ratMul_neg_pos定理附录解答

符号的乘法表

theorem ratMul_neg_pos (x y : ratRep)
    (hx : ratLtRep x ratZeroRep) (hy : ratLtRep ratZeroRep y) :
    ratLtRep (ratMulRep x y) ratZeroRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

-x 为正，ratMul_pos 给出 (-x)·y 为正；ratMul_neg_left 把它认成 -(x·y)，最后用 ratLtRep_of_neg_pos 翻回 x·y < 0。

提示 2目标形态

x < 0 → 0 < y → x·y < 0。

提示 3关键件

ratLtRep_pos_of_neg · ratMul_pos · ratMul_neg_left · ratLtRep_of_neg_pos。

查看解答 定理 · 8 行

负数乘正数得负数。-x 为正，正积 (-x)·y 为正，而它等于 -(x·y)； 最后一步把「积的相反数为正」翻成「积为负」。

theorem ratMul_neg_pos (x y : ratRep)
    (hx : ratLtRep x ratZeroRep) (hy : ratLtRep ratZeroRep y) :
    ratLtRep (ratMulRep x y) ratZeroRep :=
  ratLtRep_of_neg_pos (ratMulRep x y)
    (ratLtRep_congr_snd ratZeroRep (ratMulRep (ratNegRep x) y)
      (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x y))
      (ratMul_pos (ratNegRep x) y (ratLtRep_pos_of_neg x hx) hy)
      (ratMul_neg_left x y))
练习 5ratMul_pos_neg定理附录解答
theorem ratMul_pos_neg (x y : ratRep)
    (hx : ratLtRep ratZeroRep x) (hy : ratLtRep y ratZeroRep) :
    ratLtRep (ratMulRep x y) ratZeroRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把乘积交换成 y·x，交给上一条；再用交换律把左端点换回 x·y。

提示 2目标形态

0 < x → y < 0 → x·y < 0。

提示 3关键件

ratMul_neg_pos · ratMul_comm · ratLtRep_congr_fst。

查看解答 定理 · 6 行

正的被乘数配负的乘数，同样得负数。交换一下乘积就是上一条。

theorem ratMul_pos_neg (x y : ratRep)
    (hx : ratLtRep ratZeroRep x) (hy : ratLtRep y ratZeroRep) :
    ratLtRep (ratMulRep x y) ratZeroRep :=
  ratLtRep_congr_fst (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep y x) ratZeroRep
    (ratMul_neg_pos y x hy hx)
    (ratMul_comm x y)
练习 6ratMul_neg_neg定理附录解答
theorem ratMul_neg_neg (x y : ratRep)
    (hx : ratLtRep x ratZeroRep) (hy : ratLtRep y ratZeroRep) :
    ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratMulRep x y) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

-x 与 -y 都为正，积 (-x)·(-y) 为正；它等于 -(-(x·y))，再沿双重负号回到 x·y。

提示 2目标形态

x < 0 → y < 0 → 0 < x·y。

提示 3关键件

ratLtRep_pos_of_neg · ratMul_pos · ratMul_neg_left · ratMul_neg · ratNegRep_wd · ratNegRep_invol · ratLtRep_congr_snd。

查看解答 定理 · 19 行

两个负数相乘得正数。-x 与 -y 都为正，积 (-x)·(-y) 为正； 它等于 -(-(x·y))，再沿双重负号回到 x·y。

theorem ratMul_neg_neg (x y : ratRep)
    (hx : ratLtRep x ratZeroRep) (hy : ratLtRep y ratZeroRep) :
    ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratMulRep x y) :=
  ratLtRep_congr_snd ratZeroRep (ratMulRep (ratNegRep x) (ratNegRep y))
    (ratMulRep x y)
    (ratMul_pos (ratNegRep x) (ratNegRep y)
      (ratLtRep_pos_of_neg x hx) (ratLtRep_pos_of_neg y hy))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratMulRep (ratNegRep x) (ratNegRep y))
      (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x (ratNegRep y)))
      (ratMulRep x y)
      (ratMul_neg_left x (ratNegRep y))
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x (ratNegRep y)))
        (ratNegRep (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x y)))
        (ratMulRep x y)
        (ratNegRep_wd (ratMulRep x (ratNegRep y))
          (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x y)) (ratMul_neg x y))
        (ratNegRep_invol (ratMulRep x y))))
练习 7demo_neg_one_mul_one定理附录解答

三个读数

乘法的符号：负因子把不等号翻过来

9 道练习 · 9 份按名匹配的解答

练习 1 · ratLtRep_of_neg_pos 定理

曲线在一点取极大值，意味着往左往右走函数值都不增加。把这件事翻译成 斜率，就要知道两个差的符号：自变量往右挪是正的还是负的，函数值的改变 又跟着谁走。导数的判别最后落在一句常识上——正正得正、正负得负、 负负得正。这条常识在 ℚ 上要回到代表元，把取负与乘法的交换接起来。 证明的骨架是单元141 的 ratMul_neg：x·(-y) ~ -(x·y)。把 (-x)·y 先交换成 y·(-x)，再用这条等式收成 -(x·y)；于是「负数乘正数」化归 为「正数乘正数的积取负」。单元146 的 ratMul_pos 给出正积， ratLtRep_neg_of_pos 与 ratLtRep_pos_of_neg 负责把积的符号翻回去。 0 < -z ⇒ z < 0：把 ratLtRep_neg_of_pos 用在 -z 上，得到 -(-z) < 0，再把左端点沿 -(-z) ~ z 换回来。

theorem ratLtRep_of_neg_pos (z : ratRep)
    (h : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratNegRep z)) :
    ratLtRep z ratZeroRep :=
  ratLtRep_congr_fst z (ratNegRep (ratNegRep z)) ratZeroRep
    (ratLtRep_neg_of_pos (ratNegRep z) h)
    (ratRel_symm z (ratNegRep (ratNegRep z)) (ratNegRep_invol z))
练习 2 · ratLtRep_of_neg_neg 定理

反方向：-z < 0 ⇒ 0 < z。同样先翻一次，再把右端点换回 z。

theorem ratLtRep_of_neg_neg (z : ratRep)
    (h : ratLtRep (ratNegRep z) ratZeroRep) :
    ratLtRep ratZeroRep z :=
  ratLtRep_congr_snd ratZeroRep (ratNegRep (ratNegRep z)) z
    (ratLtRep_pos_of_neg (ratNegRep z) h)
    (ratNegRep_invol z)
练习 3 · ratMul_neg_left 定理

(-x)·y ~ -(x·y)：交换之后就是 y·(-x)，交给单元141 的 ratMul_neg， 最后把里面那个 y·x 换成 x·y。

theorem ratMul_neg_left (x y : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep (ratNegRep x) y) (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x y)) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (ratMulRep (ratNegRep x) y)
    (ratNegRep (ratMulRep y x))
    (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x y))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratMulRep (ratNegRep x) y)
      (ratMulRep y (ratNegRep x))
      (ratNegRep (ratMulRep y x))
      (ratMul_comm (ratNegRep x) y)
      (ratMul_neg y x))
    (ratNegRep_wd (ratMulRep y x) (ratMulRep x y)
      (ratRel_symm (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep y x) (ratMul_comm x y)))
练习 4 · ratMul_neg_pos 定理

负数乘正数得负数。-x 为正，正积 (-x)·y 为正，而它等于 -(x·y)； 最后一步把「积的相反数为正」翻成「积为负」。

theorem ratMul_neg_pos (x y : ratRep)
    (hx : ratLtRep x ratZeroRep) (hy : ratLtRep ratZeroRep y) :
    ratLtRep (ratMulRep x y) ratZeroRep :=
  ratLtRep_of_neg_pos (ratMulRep x y)
    (ratLtRep_congr_snd ratZeroRep (ratMulRep (ratNegRep x) y)
      (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x y))
      (ratMul_pos (ratNegRep x) y (ratLtRep_pos_of_neg x hx) hy)
      (ratMul_neg_left x y))
练习 5 · ratMul_pos_neg 定理

正的被乘数配负的乘数，同样得负数。交换一下乘积就是上一条。

theorem ratMul_pos_neg (x y : ratRep)
    (hx : ratLtRep ratZeroRep x) (hy : ratLtRep y ratZeroRep) :
    ratLtRep (ratMulRep x y) ratZeroRep :=
  ratLtRep_congr_fst (ratMulRep x y) (ratMulRep y x) ratZeroRep
    (ratMul_neg_pos y x hy hx)
    (ratMul_comm x y)
练习 6 · ratMul_neg_neg 定理

两个负数相乘得正数。-x 与 -y 都为正，积 (-x)·(-y) 为正； 它等于 -(-(x·y))，再沿双重负号回到 x·y。

theorem ratMul_neg_neg (x y : ratRep)
    (hx : ratLtRep x ratZeroRep) (hy : ratLtRep y ratZeroRep) :
    ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratMulRep x y) :=
  ratLtRep_congr_snd ratZeroRep (ratMulRep (ratNegRep x) (ratNegRep y))
    (ratMulRep x y)
    (ratMul_pos (ratNegRep x) (ratNegRep y)
      (ratLtRep_pos_of_neg x hx) (ratLtRep_pos_of_neg y hy))
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratMulRep (ratNegRep x) (ratNegRep y))
      (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x (ratNegRep y)))
      (ratMulRep x y)
      (ratMul_neg_left x (ratNegRep y))
      (ratRel_trans_gen
        (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x (ratNegRep y)))
        (ratNegRep (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x y)))
        (ratMulRep x y)
        (ratNegRep_wd (ratMulRep x (ratNegRep y))
          (ratNegRep (ratMulRep x y)) (ratMul_neg x y))
        (ratNegRep_invol (ratMulRep x y))))
练习 7 · demo_neg_one_mul_one 定理

-1 乘 1 得 -1，所以积为负。

theorem demo_neg_one_mul_one :
    ratLtRep (ratMulRep (qrep 0 1 0) (qrep 1 0 0)) ratZeroRep :=
  ratMul_neg_pos (qrep 0 1 0) (qrep 1 0 0)
    (ratLtRep_neg_of_pos (qrep 1 0 0) ratLtRep_zero_one)
    ratLtRep_zero_one
练习 8 · demo_neg_one_mul_neg_one 定理

-1 乘 -1 得 1，所以积为正。

theorem demo_neg_one_mul_neg_one :
    ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratMulRep (qrep 0 1 0) (qrep 0 1 0)) :=
  ratMul_neg_neg (qrep 0 1 0) (qrep 0 1 0)
    (ratLtRep_neg_of_pos (qrep 1 0 0) ratLtRep_zero_one)
    (ratLtRep_neg_of_pos (qrep 1 0 0) ratLtRep_zero_one)
练习 9 · demo_one_mul_one_pos 定理

两个正数的积为正：单元146 的 ratMul_pos 可以直接用在 1 上。

theorem demo_one_mul_one_pos :
    ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratMulRep (qrep 1 0 0) (qrep 1 0 0)) :=
  ratMul_pos (qrep 1 0 0) (qrep 1 0 0) ratLtRep_zero_one ratLtRep_zero_one

III.7 · 本章第 9 / 11 单元 · 全书第 148 / 171 单元

148导数的符号：曲线从切点往哪一边抬高

Unit 148 — The Sign of the Derivative: Which Side of the Tangent Point Rises

3 个定义 · 6 个定理 · 6 道练习 · 18 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

导数是一个数，它的符号应当说明曲线在切点附近往哪边抬升。若导数 L 为正，自变量从 a 往右挪一小段时，函数值的改变应当为正——曲线在 a 的右侧高于切点；若 L 为负，同样的推理在左侧给出抬高。这条单向信息正是极值判别需要的那一半：极大值点两侧都不能抬高。

证明分两层。第一层把「斜率函数的极限是 L」换成「斜率函数在去心邻域里与 L 同号」：close (q x) L L 的负侧给出 L - q x < L，两边加 q x 与 -L，就得到 0 < q x。第二层把符号传给函数值之差：x - a 的符号由 x 在 a 的哪一侧决定，两个同号的数相乘为正，再沿斜率等式 (x - a)·q x ~ f x - f a 搬到 f x - f a 上。符号表本身在单元147 已经立好，这里直接引用。

边界：只证单侧抬高，不声称整段区间上的单调；两侧的完整单调要中值定理，ℚ 上还缺上确界。函数取 ratRep → ratRep，不构造倒数，不新增公理，也不碰 kernel/front。

去心邻域里的正号

「q 在 a 的去心邻域里为正」：有一个正半径，邻域内不等于 a 的点都取正值。与单元133 的 PositiveNear 相比，这里多带了「不等于 a」的前提，因为函数极限本来就不管 a 点。

定义PositiveNearPunct声明 1
def PositiveNearPunct (q : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep) : Prop :=
  ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
    ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ¬ ratRel x a →
      ratLtRep ratZeroRep (q x)
练习 1positiveNearPunct_iff定理附录解答
theorem positiveNearPunct_iff (q : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep) :
    PositiveNearPunct q a ↔
      ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
        ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ¬ ratRel x a →
          ratLtRep ratZeroRep (q x) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

定义就是右边那串量词，两个方向都直接返回假设。

提示 2目标形态

PositiveNearPunct q a ↔ ∃ delta, 0 < delta ∧ ∀ x, close x a delta → x ≠ a → 0 < q x。

提示 3关键件

Iff.intro · 定义性展开。

查看解答 定理 · 10 行
theorem positiveNearPunct_iff (q : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep) :
    PositiveNearPunct q a ↔
      ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
        ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ¬ ratRel x a →
          ratLtRep ratZeroRep (q x) :=
  Iff.intro
    (fun (h : PositiveNearPunct q a) => h)
    (fun (h : ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
        ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ¬ ratRel x a →
          ratLtRep ratZeroRep (q x)) => h)
练习 2positiveNearPunct_of_tendsToAt定理附录解答
theorem positiveNearPunct_of_tendsToAt (q : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (hL : ratLtRep ratZeroRep L) (h : TendsToAt q a L) :
    PositiveNearPunct q a :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

ε 取 L；close_neg_lt 给出 L - q x < L；右加 q x 把左边收回 L，得到 L < L + q x；再右加 -L，左边回到零，右边收回 q x。

提示 2目标形态

0 < L → TendsToAt q a L → PositiveNearPunct q a。

提示 3关键件

close_neg_lt · ratLtRep_add_right · ratSubRep_add_self · ratAdd_neg_gen · ratAdd_assoc · ratAdd_zero。

查看解答 定理 · 47 行

ε 取 L。close_neg_lt 给出 L - q x < L；右加 q x 把左边收回 L， 得到 L < L + q x；再右加 -L，左边回到零，右边收回 q x。

theorem positiveNearPunct_of_tendsToAt (q : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (hL : ratLtRep ratZeroRep L) (h : TendsToAt q a L) :
    PositiveNearPunct q a :=
  Exists.elim (h L hL)
    (fun (delta : ratRep)
        (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
          ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ¬ ratRel x a →
            close (q x) L L) =>
      Exists.intro delta
        (And.intro (And.left hd)
          (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) (hne : ¬ ratRel x a) =>
            ratLtRep_congr_snd ratZeroRep
              (ratAddRep (ratAddRep L (q x)) (ratNegRep L)) (q x)
              (ratLtRep_congr_fst ratZeroRep
                (ratAddRep L (ratNegRep L))
                (ratAddRep (ratAddRep L (q x)) (ratNegRep L))
                (ratLtRep_add_right L (ratAddRep L (q x)) (ratNegRep L)
                  (ratLtRep_congr_fst L
                    (ratAddRep (ratSubRep L (q x)) (q x))
                    (ratAddRep L (q x))
                    (ratLtRep_add_right (ratSubRep L (q x)) L (q x)
                      (close_neg_lt (q x) L L ((And.right hd) x hx hne)))
                    (ratRel_symm
                      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep L (q x)) (q x)) L
                      (ratSubRep_add_self L (q x)))))
                (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep L (ratNegRep L)) ratZeroRep
                  (ratAdd_neg_gen L)))
              (ratRel_trans_gen
                (ratAddRep (ratAddRep L (q x)) (ratNegRep L))
                (ratAddRep (ratAddRep (q x) L) (ratNegRep L))
                (q x)
                (ratAddRep_wd_gen (ratAddRep L (q x))
                  (ratAddRep (q x) L) (ratNegRep L)
                  (ratAdd_comm L (q x)))
                (ratRel_trans_gen
                  (ratAddRep (ratAddRep (q x) L) (ratNegRep L))
                  (ratAddRep (q x) (ratAddRep L (ratNegRep L)))
                  (q x)
                  (ratAdd_assoc (q x) L (ratNegRep L))
                  (ratRel_trans_gen
                    (ratAddRep (q x) (ratAddRep L (ratNegRep L)))
                    (ratAddRep (q x) ratZeroRep)
                    (q x)
                    (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen (q x)
                      (ratAddRep L (ratNegRep L)) ratZeroRep
                      (ratAdd_neg_gen L))
                    (ratAdd_zero (q x))))))))

曲线在 a 的单侧抬高

「曲线在 a 的右侧抬高」：有一个正半径，右侧邻域里的函数值都大于 f a。

定义RisesRight声明 4
def RisesRight (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep) : Prop :=
  ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
    ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLtRep a x → close x a delta →
      ratLtRep (f a) (f x)

「曲线在 a 的左侧抬高」：左侧邻域里的函数值都大于 f a。

定义RisesLeft声明 5
def RisesLeft (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep) : Prop :=
  ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
    ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLtRep x a → close x a delta →
      ratLtRep (f a) (f x)
练习 3risesRight_of_deriv_pos定理附录解答
theorem risesRight_of_deriv_pos (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (h : HasDerivAt f a L) (hL : ratLtRep ratZeroRep L) :
    RisesRight f a :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

取斜率函数 q；符号引理给出 q x > 0，x - a > 0 由 x 在 a 右侧给出，两者相乘为正，再沿斜率等式搬到 f x - f a。

提示 2目标形态

HasDerivAt f a L → 0 < L → RisesRight f a。

提示 3关键件

positiveNearPunct_of_tendsToAt · ratMul_pos · ratLtRep_sub_pos · ratLtRep_of_sub_pos · ratMul_comm · isSlope_apply。

查看解答 定理 · 29 行

导数为正时右侧抬高。取斜率函数 q；符号引理给出 q x > 0，x - a > 0 由 x 在 a 右侧给出，两者相乘为正，再沿斜率等式搬到 f x - f a。

theorem risesRight_of_deriv_pos (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (h : HasDerivAt f a L) (hL : ratLtRep ratZeroRep L) :
    RisesRight f a :=
  Exists.elim h
    (fun (q : ratRep → ratRep)
        (hq : IsSlope f a q ∧ TendsToAt q a L) =>
      Exists.elim (Iff.mp (positiveNearPunct_iff q a)
          (positiveNearPunct_of_tendsToAt q a L hL (And.right hq)))
        (fun (delta : ratRep)
            (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
              ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ¬ ratRel x a →
                ratLtRep ratZeroRep (q x)) =>
          Exists.intro delta
            (And.intro (And.left hd)
              (fun (x : ratRep) (hax : ratLtRep a x)
                  (hx : close x a delta) =>
                ratLtRep_of_sub_pos (f x) (f a)
                  (ratLtRep_congr_snd ratZeroRep
                    (ratMulRep (q x) (ratSubRep x a))
                    (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
                    (ratMul_pos (q x) (ratSubRep x a)
                      (posAt_apply q a delta hd x hx (not_ratRel_symm a x (ratLtRep_right a x hax)))
                      (ratLtRep_sub_pos x a hax))
                    (ratRel_trans_gen
                      (ratMulRep (q x) (ratSubRep x a))
                      (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (q x))
                      (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
                      (ratMul_comm (q x) (ratSubRep x a))
                      (isSlope_apply f a q (And.left hq) x (not_ratRel_symm a x (ratLtRep_right a x hax)))))))))
练习 4risesLeft_of_deriv_neg定理附录解答
theorem risesLeft_of_deriv_neg (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (h : HasDerivAt f a L) (hL : ratLtRep L ratZeroRep) :
    RisesLeft f a :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把 -q 看成斜率函数的极限 -L：它是正的，符号引理给出 -(q x) > 0，也就是 q x < 0。x 在 a 左侧时 x - a < 0，两个负数相乘为正，再沿斜率等式搬到 f x - f a。

提示 2目标形态

HasDerivAt f a L → L < 0 → RisesLeft f a。

提示 3关键件

tendsToAt_neg · positiveNearPunct_of_tendsToAt · ratLtRep_of_neg_pos · ratMul_neg_neg · ratNeg_sub_swap · ratLtRep_of_sub_pos。

查看解答 定理 · 39 行

导数为负时左侧抬高。把 -q 看成斜率函数的极限 -L：它是正的， 符号引理给出 -(q x) > 0，也就是 q x < 0。x 在 a 左侧时 x - a < 0， 两个负数相乘为正，再沿斜率等式搬到 f x - f a。

theorem risesLeft_of_deriv_neg (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (h : HasDerivAt f a L) (hL : ratLtRep L ratZeroRep) :
    RisesLeft f a :=
  Exists.elim h
    (fun (q : ratRep → ratRep)
        (hq : IsSlope f a q ∧ TendsToAt q a L) =>
      Exists.elim
        (Iff.mp (positiveNearPunct_iff (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (q x)) a)
          (positiveNearPunct_of_tendsToAt
            (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (q x)) a (ratNegRep L)
            (ratLtRep_pos_of_neg L hL) (tendsToAt_neg q a L (And.right hq))))
        (fun (delta : ratRep)
            (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
              ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ¬ ratRel x a →
                ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratNegRep (q x))) =>
          Exists.intro delta
            (And.intro (And.left hd)
              (fun (x : ratRep) (hxa : ratLtRep x a)
                  (hx : close x a delta) =>
                ratLtRep_of_sub_pos (f x) (f a)
                  (ratLtRep_congr_snd ratZeroRep
                    (ratMulRep (q x) (ratSubRep x a))
                    (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
                    (ratMul_neg_neg (q x) (ratSubRep x a)
                      (ratLtRep_of_neg_pos (q x)
                        (posAt_apply (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (q x)) a delta hd x hx
                          (ratLtRep_right x a hxa)))
                      (ratLtRep_congr_fst (ratSubRep x a)
                        (ratNegRep (ratSubRep a x)) ratZeroRep
                        (ratLtRep_neg_of_pos (ratSubRep a x)
                          (ratLtRep_sub_pos a x hxa))
                        (ratRel_symm (ratNegRep (ratSubRep a x)) (ratSubRep x a)
                          (ratNeg_sub_swap a x))))
                    (ratRel_trans_gen
                      (ratMulRep (q x) (ratSubRep x a))
                      (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (q x))
                      (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
                      (ratMul_comm (q x) (ratSubRep x a))
                      (isSlope_apply f a q (And.left hq) x (ratLtRep_right x a hxa))))))))
练习 5demo_id_rises_right定理附录解答

两个读数

    RisesRight (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

  risesRight_of_deriv_pos (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep ratOneRep

    (hasDerivAt_neg (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep ratOneRep
      (hasDerivAt_id ratZeroRep))
    (ratLtRep_neg_of_pos ratOneRep ratLtRep_zero_one)

导数的符号：曲线从切点往哪一边抬高

6 道练习 · 6 份按名匹配的解答 · 4 个解答独有声明

练习 1 · positiveNearPunct_iff 定理
theorem positiveNearPunct_iff (q : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep) :
    PositiveNearPunct q a ↔
      ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
        ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ¬ ratRel x a →
          ratLtRep ratZeroRep (q x) :=
  Iff.intro
    (fun (h : PositiveNearPunct q a) => h)
    (fun (h : ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
        ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ¬ ratRel x a →
          ratLtRep ratZeroRep (q x)) => h)
练习 2 · positiveNearPunct_of_tendsToAt 定理

ε 取 L。close_neg_lt 给出 L - q x < L；右加 q x 把左边收回 L， 得到 L < L + q x；再右加 -L，左边回到零，右边收回 q x。

theorem positiveNearPunct_of_tendsToAt (q : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (hL : ratLtRep ratZeroRep L) (h : TendsToAt q a L) :
    PositiveNearPunct q a :=
  Exists.elim (h L hL)
    (fun (delta : ratRep)
        (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
          ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ¬ ratRel x a →
            close (q x) L L) =>
      Exists.intro delta
        (And.intro (And.left hd)
          (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) (hne : ¬ ratRel x a) =>
            ratLtRep_congr_snd ratZeroRep
              (ratAddRep (ratAddRep L (q x)) (ratNegRep L)) (q x)
              (ratLtRep_congr_fst ratZeroRep
                (ratAddRep L (ratNegRep L))
                (ratAddRep (ratAddRep L (q x)) (ratNegRep L))
                (ratLtRep_add_right L (ratAddRep L (q x)) (ratNegRep L)
                  (ratLtRep_congr_fst L
                    (ratAddRep (ratSubRep L (q x)) (q x))
                    (ratAddRep L (q x))
                    (ratLtRep_add_right (ratSubRep L (q x)) L (q x)
                      (close_neg_lt (q x) L L ((And.right hd) x hx hne)))
                    (ratRel_symm
                      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep L (q x)) (q x)) L
                      (ratSubRep_add_self L (q x)))))
                (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep L (ratNegRep L)) ratZeroRep
                  (ratAdd_neg_gen L)))
              (ratRel_trans_gen
                (ratAddRep (ratAddRep L (q x)) (ratNegRep L))
                (ratAddRep (ratAddRep (q x) L) (ratNegRep L))
                (q x)
                (ratAddRep_wd_gen (ratAddRep L (q x))
                  (ratAddRep (q x) L) (ratNegRep L)
                  (ratAdd_comm L (q x)))
                (ratRel_trans_gen
                  (ratAddRep (ratAddRep (q x) L) (ratNegRep L))
                  (ratAddRep (q x) (ratAddRep L (ratNegRep L)))
                  (q x)
                  (ratAdd_assoc (q x) L (ratNegRep L))
                  (ratRel_trans_gen
                    (ratAddRep (q x) (ratAddRep L (ratNegRep L)))
                    (ratAddRep (q x) ratZeroRep)
                    (q x)
                    (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen (q x)
                      (ratAddRep L (ratNegRep L)) ratZeroRep
                      (ratAdd_neg_gen L))
                    (ratAdd_zero (q x))))))))
练习 3 · risesRight_of_deriv_pos 定理

导数为正时右侧抬高。取斜率函数 q；符号引理给出 q x > 0，x - a > 0 由 x 在 a 右侧给出，两者相乘为正，再沿斜率等式搬到 f x - f a。

theorem risesRight_of_deriv_pos (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (h : HasDerivAt f a L) (hL : ratLtRep ratZeroRep L) :
    RisesRight f a :=
  Exists.elim h
    (fun (q : ratRep → ratRep)
        (hq : IsSlope f a q ∧ TendsToAt q a L) =>
      Exists.elim (Iff.mp (positiveNearPunct_iff q a)
          (positiveNearPunct_of_tendsToAt q a L hL (And.right hq)))
        (fun (delta : ratRep)
            (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
              ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ¬ ratRel x a →
                ratLtRep ratZeroRep (q x)) =>
          Exists.intro delta
            (And.intro (And.left hd)
              (fun (x : ratRep) (hax : ratLtRep a x)
                  (hx : close x a delta) =>
                ratLtRep_of_sub_pos (f x) (f a)
                  (ratLtRep_congr_snd ratZeroRep
                    (ratMulRep (q x) (ratSubRep x a))
                    (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
                    (ratMul_pos (q x) (ratSubRep x a)
                      (posAt_apply q a delta hd x hx (not_ratRel_symm a x (ratLtRep_right a x hax)))
                      (ratLtRep_sub_pos x a hax))
                    (ratRel_trans_gen
                      (ratMulRep (q x) (ratSubRep x a))
                      (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (q x))
                      (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
                      (ratMul_comm (q x) (ratSubRep x a))
                      (isSlope_apply f a q (And.left hq) x (not_ratRel_symm a x (ratLtRep_right a x hax)))))))))
练习 4 · risesLeft_of_deriv_neg 定理

导数为负时左侧抬高。把 -q 看成斜率函数的极限 -L：它是正的， 符号引理给出 -(q x) > 0，也就是 q x < 0。x 在 a 左侧时 x - a < 0， 两个负数相乘为正，再沿斜率等式搬到 f x - f a。

theorem risesLeft_of_deriv_neg (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (h : HasDerivAt f a L) (hL : ratLtRep L ratZeroRep) :
    RisesLeft f a :=
  Exists.elim h
    (fun (q : ratRep → ratRep)
        (hq : IsSlope f a q ∧ TendsToAt q a L) =>
      Exists.elim
        (Iff.mp (positiveNearPunct_iff (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (q x)) a)
          (positiveNearPunct_of_tendsToAt
            (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (q x)) a (ratNegRep L)
            (ratLtRep_pos_of_neg L hL) (tendsToAt_neg q a L (And.right hq))))
        (fun (delta : ratRep)
            (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
              ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ¬ ratRel x a →
                ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratNegRep (q x))) =>
          Exists.intro delta
            (And.intro (And.left hd)
              (fun (x : ratRep) (hxa : ratLtRep x a)
                  (hx : close x a delta) =>
                ratLtRep_of_sub_pos (f x) (f a)
                  (ratLtRep_congr_snd ratZeroRep
                    (ratMulRep (q x) (ratSubRep x a))
                    (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
                    (ratMul_neg_neg (q x) (ratSubRep x a)
                      (ratLtRep_of_neg_pos (q x)
                        (posAt_apply (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (q x)) a delta hd x hx
                          (ratLtRep_right x a hxa)))
                      (ratLtRep_congr_fst (ratSubRep x a)
                        (ratNegRep (ratSubRep a x)) ratZeroRep
                        (ratLtRep_neg_of_pos (ratSubRep a x)
                          (ratLtRep_sub_pos a x hxa))
                        (ratRel_symm (ratNegRep (ratSubRep a x)) (ratSubRep x a)
                          (ratNeg_sub_swap a x))))
                    (ratRel_trans_gen
                      (ratMulRep (q x) (ratSubRep x a))
                      (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (q x))
                      (ratSubRep (f x) (f a))
                      (ratMul_comm (q x) (ratSubRep x a))
                      (isSlope_apply f a q (And.left hq) x (ratLtRep_right x a hxa))))))))
练习 5 · demo_id_rises_right 定理

恒等函数在零点的导数为 1，所以曲线在零点右侧抬高。

theorem demo_id_rises_right :
    RisesRight (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep :=
  risesRight_of_deriv_pos (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep ratOneRep
    (hasDerivAt_id ratZeroRep) ratLtRep_zero_one
练习 6 · demo_neg_id_rises_left 定理

取负的恒等函数在零点的导数为 -1，所以曲线在零点左侧抬高。

theorem demo_neg_id_rises_left :
    RisesLeft (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep x) ratZeroRep :=
  risesLeft_of_deriv_neg (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep x) ratZeroRep
    (ratNegRep ratOneRep)
    (hasDerivAt_neg (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep ratOneRep
      (hasDerivAt_id ratZeroRep))
    (ratLtRep_neg_of_pos ratOneRep ratLtRep_zero_one)
补充 · not_ratRel_symm 定理
theorem not_ratRel_symm (x y : ratRep) (h : ¬ ratRel x y) :
    ¬ ratRel y x :=
  fun (hyx : ratRel y x) => h (ratRel_symm y x hyx)
补充 · isSlope_apply 定理
theorem isSlope_apply (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (q : ratRep → ratRep) (h : IsSlope f a q) (x : ratRep)
    (hne : ¬ ratRel x a) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep (ratSubRep x a) (q x))
      (ratSubRep (f x) (f a)) :=
  (Iff.mp (isSlope_iff f a q) h) x hne
补充 · posAt_apply 定理
theorem posAt_apply (r : ratRep → ratRep) (a delta : ratRep)
    (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ¬ ratRel x a →
        ratLtRep ratZeroRep (r x))
    (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) (hne : ¬ ratRel x a) :
    ratLtRep ratZeroRep (r x) :=
  (And.right hd) x hx hne
补充 · ratNeg_sub_swap 定理
theorem ratNeg_sub_swap (x y : ratRep) :
    ratRel (ratNegRep (ratSubRep x y)) (ratSubRep y x) :=
  ratRel_trans_gen
    (ratNegRep (ratSubRep x y))
    (ratAddRep (ratNegRep x) y)
    (ratSubRep y x)
    (ratRel_trans_gen
      (ratNegRep (ratSubRep x y))
      (ratAddRep (ratNegRep x) (ratNegRep (ratNegRep y)))
      (ratAddRep (ratNegRep x) y)
      (ratNeg_sub x y)
      (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen (ratNegRep x)
        (ratNegRep (ratNegRep y)) y (ratNegRep_invol y)))
    (ratAdd_comm (ratNegRep x) y)

III.7 · 本章第 10 / 11 单元 · 全书第 149 / 171 单元

149极值点的导数为零：曲线在最高处没有斜率

Unit 149 — The Derivative Vanishes at Interior Extrema

2 个定义 · 9 个定理 · 9 道练习 · 27 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

山坡的最高点处，切线若还是斜的，曲线就会有一侧继续往上走，那里也就不再是最高点。换成函数的语言：在局部极大值点附近，函数值不能随 x 往右增大，也不能随 x 往左增大；单元148 已经把导数的正负翻译成曲线往哪一侧抬高，于是一个局部极大值点把导数的两个符号都排除，只剩零。

定义先把「局部」写成半径为 delta 的邻域：邻域内每一点的函数值都不超过 f a，就是局部极大；反过来每一点都不低于 f a，就是局部极小。邻域同时管两侧，所以 a 必须是定义域的内点；端点上的极值不在讨论范围内。

证明只做极大。若 L > 0，risesRight_of_deriv_pos 在 a 的右侧给出一点 x，使 f a < f x，与局部极大的 f x ≤ f a 冲突；若 L < 0，risesLeft_of_deriv_neg 在左侧给出同样的冲突。两个严格不等式都被排除，再用 ℚ 的序全性把 0 ≤ L 与 L ≤ 0 两支各收到 0 ~ L 或 L ~ 0 上。极小值不重做一遍：把曲线关于 x 轴翻过来，极小变极大，导数变成 -L，而 -L 为零与 L 为零是同一件事。

边界：只讨论内点上的双侧极值，单侧极值与整段区间上的最值需要别的工具。恒等函数在零点可导，却既不是局部极大也不是局部极小，这条反例说明可导本身不保证任何极值。

局部极大与局部极小

「局部极大」：有一个正半径，邻域内每一点的函数值都不超过 f a。邻域不排除 a 本身，这与单元133 的保号邻域不同；把 a 算进来不影响结论。

定义LocalMaxAt声明 1
def LocalMaxAt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep) : Prop :=
  ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
    ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLeRep (f x) (f a)

「局部极小」：邻域内每一点的函数值都不低于 f a。

定义LocalMinAt声明 2
def LocalMinAt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep) : Prop :=
  ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
    ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLeRep (f a) (f x)
练习 1localMaxAt_iff定理附录解答
theorem localMaxAt_iff (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep) :
    LocalMaxAt f a ↔
      ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
        ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLeRep (f x) (f a) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

定义就是右边那串量词，两个方向都直接返回假设。

提示 2目标形态

LocalMaxAt f a ↔ ∃ delta, 0 < delta ∧ ∀ x, close x a delta → f x ≤ f a。

提示 3关键件

练习 3not_risesRight_of_localMax定理附录解答

极大值把两个方向都挡住

theorem not_risesRight_of_localMax (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hmax : LocalMaxAt f a) : ¬ RisesRight f a :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

反证。取极大邻域与抬高邻域的较小半径 d，在 a 右侧取 x = a + d/2。它同时落在两个邻域里：抬高给出 f a < f x，极大给出 f x ≤ f a；反对称性把这两个不等式合成 f a ~ f x，与严格小的第二分量冲突。

提示 2目标形态

LocalMaxAt f a → ¬ RisesRight f a。

提示 3关键件

exists_min_pos · close_add_right · close_mono_eps · ratLtRep_self_add_pos · ratLeRep_antisymm · And.right。

查看解答 定理 · 14 行

把局部极大的存在性与 RisesRight 的存在性各拆开，半径和全称命题交给 上面的冲突引理。

theorem not_risesRight_of_localMax (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hmax : LocalMaxAt f a) : ¬ RisesRight f a :=
  fun (hr : RisesRight f a) =>
    Exists.elim hmax
      (fun (dmax : ratRep)
          (hdmax : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dmax ∧
            ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a dmax → ratLeRep (f x) (f a)) =>
        Exists.elim hr
          (fun (dr : ratRep)
              (hdr : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dr ∧
                ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLtRep a x → close x a dr →
                  ratLtRep (f a) (f x)) =>
            risesRight_conflict f a dmax dr (And.left hdmax)
              (And.right hdmax) (And.left hdr) (And.right hdr)))
练习 4not_risesLeft_of_localMax定理附录解答
theorem not_risesLeft_of_localMax (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hmax : LocalMaxAt f a) : ¬ RisesLeft f a :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

与右侧同构。这次在 a 左侧取 x = a - d/2，close_sub_half 把它送进邻域，ratLtRep_self_sub_pos 给出 x < a；剩下的一步不变。

theorem not_risesLeft_of_localMax (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hmax : LocalMaxAt f a) : ¬ RisesLeft f a :=
  fun (hr : RisesLeft f a) =>
    Exists.elim hmax
      (fun (dmax : ratRep)
          (hdmax : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dmax ∧
            ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a dmax → ratLeRep (f x) (f a)) =>
        Exists.elim hr
          (fun (dr : ratRep)
              (hdr : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dr ∧
                ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLtRep x a → close x a dr →
                  ratLtRep (f a) (f x)) =>
            risesLeft_conflict f a dmax dr (And.left hdmax)
              (And.right hdmax) (And.left hdr) (And.right hdr)))
练习 5deriv_zero_of_localMax定理附录解答

导数只能是零

theorem deriv_zero_of_localMax (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (h : HasDerivAt f a L) (hmax : LocalMaxAt f a) :
    ratRel L ratZeroRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

若 0 < L，右侧抬高与上一条的「不抬高」冲突；若 L < 0，左侧抬高同样冲突。于是 ¬ 0 < L 与 ¬ L < 0。序全性给 0 ≤ L 或 L ≤ 0，每支再用 ratLeRep_eq_or_lt 拆成相等或严格小，严格小那一支撞上刚得到的否定，相等那一支就是目标（必要时把等式两端对调）。

提示 2目标形态

HasDerivAt f a L → LocalMaxAt f a → L ~ 0。

提示 3关键件

risesRight_of_deriv_pos · risesLeft_of_deriv_neg · ratLeRep_total · ratLeRep_eq_or_lt · ratRel_symm · False.elim。

查看解答 定理 · 19 行

两个严格不等式先立起来。收口时用序全性把 0 ≤ L 与 L ≤ 0 分成两支： 每一支里 ratLeRep_eq_or_lt 再分成「相等」与「严格小」，严格小那一支 正好撞上刚得到的否定。四个分支合并起来，剩下的只有 L ~ 0。

theorem deriv_zero_of_localMax (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (h : HasDerivAt f a L) (hmax : LocalMaxAt f a) :
    ratRel L ratZeroRep :=
  @Or.elim (ratLeRep ratZeroRep L) (ratLeRep L ratZeroRep) (ratRel L ratZeroRep)
    (fun (hge : ratLeRep ratZeroRep L) =>
      @Or.elim (ratRel ratZeroRep L) (ratLtRep ratZeroRep L) (ratRel L ratZeroRep)
        (fun (heq : ratRel ratZeroRep L) => ratRel_symm ratZeroRep L heq)
        (fun (hlt : ratLtRep ratZeroRep L) =>
          False.elim (not_risesRight_of_localMax f a hmax
            (risesRight_of_deriv_pos f a L h hlt)))
        (ratLeRep_eq_or_lt ratZeroRep L hge))
    (fun (hle : ratLeRep L ratZeroRep) =>
      @Or.elim (ratRel L ratZeroRep) (ratLtRep L ratZeroRep) (ratRel L ratZeroRep)
        (fun (heq : ratRel L ratZeroRep) => heq)
        (fun (hlt : ratLtRep L ratZeroRep) =>
          False.elim (not_risesLeft_of_localMax f a hmax
            (risesLeft_of_deriv_neg f a L h hlt)))
        (ratLeRep_eq_or_lt L ratZeroRep hle))
    (ratLeRep_total ratZeroRep L)
练习 6localMaxAt_neg_of_localMin定理附录解答

极小值：把曲线翻过来

theorem localMaxAt_neg_of_localMin (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (h : LocalMinAt f a) : LocalMaxAt (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (f x)) a :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

极小说 f a ≤ f x；两边取负，不等号翻成 -f x ≤ -f a，这正是 -f 的局部极大，半径原样沿用。

提示 2目标形态

LocalMinAt f a → LocalMaxAt (fun x => -f x) a。

提示 3关键件

ratLeRep_neg_gen · Exists.intro · And.intro。

查看解答 定理 · 10 行

极小给 f a ≤ f x。取负把不等号翻成 -f x ≤ -f a，半径不动，就是 -f 的局部极大。

theorem localMaxAt_neg_of_localMin (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (h : LocalMinAt f a) : LocalMaxAt (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (f x)) a :=
  Exists.elim h
    (fun (delta : ratRep)
        (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
          ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLeRep (f a) (f x)) =>
      Exists.intro delta
        (And.intro (And.left hd)
          (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) =>
            ratLeRep_neg_gen (f a) (f x) ((And.right hd) x hx))))
练习 7deriv_zero_of_localMin定理附录解答
theorem deriv_zero_of_localMin (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (h : HasDerivAt f a L) (hmin : LocalMinAt f a) :
    ratRel L ratZeroRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

对 -f 用极大版本，得到 -L ~ 0；两边同时取负，-(-L) 收回 L、-0 收回 0，就有 L ~ 0。

提示 2目标形态

HasDerivAt f a L → LocalMinAt f a → L ~ 0。

提示 3关键件

hasDerivAt_neg · deriv_zero_of_localMax · ratNegRep_wd · ratNegRep_invol · ratRel_symm · ratRel_trans_gen。

查看解答 定理 · 11 行

对 -f 用极大版本，得到 -L ~ 0。两边同时取负：-(-L) 沿 ratNegRep_invol 收回 L，-0 归约成 0；再把取负后的等式接到目标上。

theorem deriv_zero_of_localMin (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (h : HasDerivAt f a L) (hmin : LocalMinAt f a) :
    ratRel L ratZeroRep :=
  ratRel_trans_gen L (ratNegRep ratZeroRep) ratZeroRep
    (ratRel_trans_gen L (ratNegRep (ratNegRep L)) (ratNegRep ratZeroRep)
      (ratRel_symm (ratNegRep (ratNegRep L)) L (ratNegRep_invol L))
      (ratNegRep_wd (ratNegRep L) ratZeroRep
        (deriv_zero_of_localMax (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (f x)) a
          (ratNegRep L) (hasDerivAt_neg f a L h)
          (localMaxAt_neg_of_localMin f a hmin))))
    (ratRel_refl ratZeroRep)
练习 8demo_id_not_localMax定理附录解答

两个反例

theorem demo_id_not_localMax : ¬ LocalMaxAt (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

任取极大邻域的半径 delta，右侧点 0 + delta/2 落在邻域里。极大要求它 ≤ 0，而它严格大于 0；反对称性给出 0 ~ (0 + delta/2)，与严格小的第二分量冲突。

提示 2目标形态

¬ LocalMaxAt (fun x => x) 0。

提示 3关键件

close_add_right · ratLtRep_self_add_pos · ratLeRep_antisymm · not_ratRel_symm · And.right。

查看解答 定理 · 18 行

恒等函数在零点：任取半径 delta，右侧点 0 + delta/2 落在邻域里，极大 要求它不超过 0；它本身严格大于 0，两条不等式只能合成一个假等式。

theorem demo_id_not_localMax : ¬ LocalMaxAt (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep :=
  fun (h : LocalMaxAt (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep) =>
    Exists.elim h
      (fun (delta : ratRep)
          (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
            ∀ (x : ratRep), close x ratZeroRep delta → ratLeRep x ratZeroRep) =>
        (not_ratRel_symm ratZeroRep (ratAddRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta))
          (And.right (ratLtRep_self_add_pos ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta)
            (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd)))))
          (ratLeRep_antisymm (ratAddRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta)) ratZeroRep
            (le_apply (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep delta hd
              (ratAddRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta))
              (close_add_right ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta) delta
                (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd))
                (ratHalf_lt_self delta (And.left hd))))
            (ratLtRep_le ratZeroRep (ratAddRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta))
              (ratLtRep_self_add_pos ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta)
                (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd))))))
练习 9demo_id_not_localMin定理附录解答
theorem demo_id_not_localMin : ¬ LocalMinAt (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把右侧换成左侧：点 0 - delta/2 落在邻域里，极小要求它 ≥ 0，而它严格小于 0；反对称性给出它与 0 相等，与严格小的第二分量冲突。

提示 2目标形态

¬ LocalMinAt (fun x => x) 0。

提示 3关键件

close_sub_half · ratLtRep_self_sub_pos · ratLeRep_antisymm · And.right。

查看解答 定理 · 14 行

极小那一支把右侧点换成左侧点 0 - delta/2：极小要求它不低于 0，它却 严格小于 0。

theorem demo_id_not_localMin : ¬ LocalMinAt (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep :=
  fun (h : LocalMinAt (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep) =>
    Exists.elim h
      (fun (delta : ratRep)
          (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
            ∀ (x : ratRep), close x ratZeroRep delta → ratLeRep ratZeroRep x) =>
        (And.right (ratLtRep_self_sub_pos ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta)
            (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd))))
          (ratLeRep_antisymm (ratSubRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta)) ratZeroRep
            (And.left (ratLtRep_self_sub_pos ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta)
              (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd))))
            (ge_apply (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep delta hd
              (ratSubRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta))
              (close_sub_half ratZeroRep delta (And.left hd)))))

极值点的导数为零：曲线在最高处没有斜率

9 道练习 · 9 份按名匹配的解答 · 6 个解答独有声明

练习 1 · localMaxAt_iff 定理
theorem localMaxAt_iff (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep) :
    LocalMaxAt f a ↔
      ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
        ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLeRep (f x) (f a) :=
  Iff.intro
    (fun (h : LocalMaxAt f a) => h)
    (fun (h : ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
        ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLeRep (f x) (f a)) => h)
练习 2 · localMinAt_iff 定理

        ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLeRep (f a) (f x) :=

        ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLeRep (f a) (f x)) => h)
练习 3 · not_risesRight_of_localMax 定理

把局部极大的存在性与 RisesRight 的存在性各拆开，半径和全称命题交给 上面的冲突引理。

theorem not_risesRight_of_localMax (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hmax : LocalMaxAt f a) : ¬ RisesRight f a :=
  fun (hr : RisesRight f a) =>
    Exists.elim hmax
      (fun (dmax : ratRep)
          (hdmax : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dmax ∧
            ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a dmax → ratLeRep (f x) (f a)) =>
        Exists.elim hr
          (fun (dr : ratRep)
              (hdr : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dr ∧
                ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLtRep a x → close x a dr →
                  ratLtRep (f a) (f x)) =>
            risesRight_conflict f a dmax dr (And.left hdmax)
              (And.right hdmax) (And.left hdr) (And.right hdr)))
练习 4 · not_risesLeft_of_localMax 定理
theorem not_risesLeft_of_localMax (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hmax : LocalMaxAt f a) : ¬ RisesLeft f a :=
  fun (hr : RisesLeft f a) =>
    Exists.elim hmax
      (fun (dmax : ratRep)
          (hdmax : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dmax ∧
            ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a dmax → ratLeRep (f x) (f a)) =>
        Exists.elim hr
          (fun (dr : ratRep)
              (hdr : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dr ∧
                ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLtRep x a → close x a dr →
                  ratLtRep (f a) (f x)) =>
            risesLeft_conflict f a dmax dr (And.left hdmax)
              (And.right hdmax) (And.left hdr) (And.right hdr)))
练习 5 · deriv_zero_of_localMax 定理

两个严格不等式先立起来。收口时用序全性把 0 ≤ L 与 L ≤ 0 分成两支： 每一支里 ratLeRep_eq_or_lt 再分成「相等」与「严格小」，严格小那一支 正好撞上刚得到的否定。四个分支合并起来，剩下的只有 L ~ 0。

theorem deriv_zero_of_localMax (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (h : HasDerivAt f a L) (hmax : LocalMaxAt f a) :
    ratRel L ratZeroRep :=
  @Or.elim (ratLeRep ratZeroRep L) (ratLeRep L ratZeroRep) (ratRel L ratZeroRep)
    (fun (hge : ratLeRep ratZeroRep L) =>
      @Or.elim (ratRel ratZeroRep L) (ratLtRep ratZeroRep L) (ratRel L ratZeroRep)
        (fun (heq : ratRel ratZeroRep L) => ratRel_symm ratZeroRep L heq)
        (fun (hlt : ratLtRep ratZeroRep L) =>
          False.elim (not_risesRight_of_localMax f a hmax
            (risesRight_of_deriv_pos f a L h hlt)))
        (ratLeRep_eq_or_lt ratZeroRep L hge))
    (fun (hle : ratLeRep L ratZeroRep) =>
      @Or.elim (ratRel L ratZeroRep) (ratLtRep L ratZeroRep) (ratRel L ratZeroRep)
        (fun (heq : ratRel L ratZeroRep) => heq)
        (fun (hlt : ratLtRep L ratZeroRep) =>
          False.elim (not_risesLeft_of_localMax f a hmax
            (risesLeft_of_deriv_neg f a L h hlt)))
        (ratLeRep_eq_or_lt L ratZeroRep hle))
    (ratLeRep_total ratZeroRep L)
练习 6 · localMaxAt_neg_of_localMin 定理

极小给 f a ≤ f x。取负把不等号翻成 -f x ≤ -f a，半径不动，就是 -f 的局部极大。

theorem localMaxAt_neg_of_localMin (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (h : LocalMinAt f a) : LocalMaxAt (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (f x)) a :=
  Exists.elim h
    (fun (delta : ratRep)
        (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
          ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLeRep (f a) (f x)) =>
      Exists.intro delta
        (And.intro (And.left hd)
          (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) =>
            ratLeRep_neg_gen (f a) (f x) ((And.right hd) x hx))))
练习 7 · deriv_zero_of_localMin 定理

对 -f 用极大版本，得到 -L ~ 0。两边同时取负：-(-L) 沿 ratNegRep_invol 收回 L，-0 归约成 0；再把取负后的等式接到目标上。

theorem deriv_zero_of_localMin (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (h : HasDerivAt f a L) (hmin : LocalMinAt f a) :
    ratRel L ratZeroRep :=
  ratRel_trans_gen L (ratNegRep ratZeroRep) ratZeroRep
    (ratRel_trans_gen L (ratNegRep (ratNegRep L)) (ratNegRep ratZeroRep)
      (ratRel_symm (ratNegRep (ratNegRep L)) L (ratNegRep_invol L))
      (ratNegRep_wd (ratNegRep L) ratZeroRep
        (deriv_zero_of_localMax (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (f x)) a
          (ratNegRep L) (hasDerivAt_neg f a L h)
          (localMaxAt_neg_of_localMin f a hmin))))
    (ratRel_refl ratZeroRep)
练习 8 · demo_id_not_localMax 定理

恒等函数在零点：任取半径 delta，右侧点 0 + delta/2 落在邻域里，极大 要求它不超过 0；它本身严格大于 0，两条不等式只能合成一个假等式。

theorem demo_id_not_localMax : ¬ LocalMaxAt (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep :=
  fun (h : LocalMaxAt (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep) =>
    Exists.elim h
      (fun (delta : ratRep)
          (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
            ∀ (x : ratRep), close x ratZeroRep delta → ratLeRep x ratZeroRep) =>
        (not_ratRel_symm ratZeroRep (ratAddRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta))
          (And.right (ratLtRep_self_add_pos ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta)
            (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd)))))
          (ratLeRep_antisymm (ratAddRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta)) ratZeroRep
            (le_apply (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep delta hd
              (ratAddRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta))
              (close_add_right ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta) delta
                (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd))
                (ratHalf_lt_self delta (And.left hd))))
            (ratLtRep_le ratZeroRep (ratAddRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta))
              (ratLtRep_self_add_pos ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta)
                (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd))))))
练习 9 · demo_id_not_localMin 定理

theorem demo_id_not_localMin : ¬ LocalMinAt (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep :=

补充 · le_conflict 定理
theorem le_conflict (p q : ratRep) (hlt : ratLtRep p q)
    (hle : ratLeRep q p) : False :=
  (And.right hlt) (ratLeRep_antisymm p q (And.left hlt) hle)
补充 · risesRight_conflict 定理
theorem risesRight_conflict (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a dmax dr : ratRep)
    (hdmax : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dmax)
    (hmax : ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a dmax → ratLeRep (f x) (f a))
    (hdr : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dr)
    (hrise : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLtRep a x → close x a dr →
      ratLtRep (f a) (f x)) :
    False :=
  Exists.elim (exists_min_pos dmax dr hdmax hdr)
    (fun (d : ratRep)
        (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep d ∧ ratLeRep d dmax ∧ ratLeRep d dr) =>
      le_conflict (f a) (f (ratAddRep a (ratHalf d)))
        (hrise (ratAddRep a (ratHalf d))
          (ratLtRep_self_add_pos a (ratHalf d)
            (ratLtRep_half d (And.left hd)))
          (close_mono_eps (ratAddRep a (ratHalf d)) a d dr
            (close_add_right a (ratHalf d) d (ratLtRep_half d (And.left hd))
              (ratHalf_lt_self d (And.left hd)))
            (And.right (And.right hd))))
        (hmax (ratAddRep a (ratHalf d))
          (close_mono_eps (ratAddRep a (ratHalf d)) a d dmax
            (close_add_right a (ratHalf d) d (ratLtRep_half d (And.left hd))
              (ratHalf_lt_self d (And.left hd)))
            (And.left (And.right hd)))))
补充 · close_sub_half 定理
theorem close_sub_half (a d : ratRep) (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep d) :
    close (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)) a d :=
  close_of_le_lb (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)) a d (ratHalf d)
    (ratLtRep_le (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)) a
      (ratLtRep_self_sub_pos a (ratHalf d) (ratLtRep_half d hd)))
    (ratLeRep_of_ratRel a (ratAddRep (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)) (ratHalf d))
      (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)) (ratHalf d)) a
        (ratSubRep_add_self a (ratHalf d))))
    (ratHalf_lt_self d hd)
补充 · risesLeft_conflict 定理
theorem risesLeft_conflict (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a dmax dr : ratRep)
    (hdmax : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dmax)
    (hmax : ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a dmax → ratLeRep (f x) (f a))
    (hdr : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dr)
    (hrise : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLtRep x a → close x a dr →
      ratLtRep (f a) (f x)) :
    False :=
  Exists.elim (exists_min_pos dmax dr hdmax hdr)
    (fun (d : ratRep)
        (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep d ∧ ratLeRep d dmax ∧ ratLeRep d dr) =>
      le_conflict (f a) (f (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)))
        (hrise (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d))
          (ratLtRep_self_sub_pos a (ratHalf d)
            (ratLtRep_half d (And.left hd)))
          (close_mono_eps (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)) a d dr
            (close_sub_half a d (And.left hd))
            (And.right (And.right hd))))
        (hmax (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d))
          (close_mono_eps (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)) a d dmax
            (close_sub_half a d (And.left hd))
            (And.left (And.right hd)))))
补充 · le_apply 定理
theorem le_apply (r : ratRep → ratRep) (a delta : ratRep)
    (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLeRep (r x) (r a))
    (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) : ratLeRep (r x) (r a) :=
  (And.right hd) x hx
补充 · ge_apply 定理
theorem ge_apply (r : ratRep → ratRep) (a delta : ratRep)

      ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLeRep (r a) (r x))
    (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) : ratLeRep (r a) (r x) :=
  (And.right hd) x hx

III.7 · 本章第 11 / 11 单元 · 全书第 150 / 171 单元

150单调与导数的符号：不下降的曲线不会往下斜

Unit 150 — Monotonicity and the Sign of the Derivative

2 个定义 · 9 个定理 · 9 道练习 · 27 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

曲线在一点附近如果从不下降，它的切线能有多陡？至少不能是负的：负斜率意味着从切点往左走一小段，曲线反而更高（单元148 的左侧抬高），这与不下降直接冲突。反方向——导数非负推出函数不下降——要中值定理，本阶段还没有；这里只做前一方向。

定义只要求局部：存在一个正半径，邻域内左边的点函数值不超过 a 点的值，右边的点函数值不低于 a 点的值，就叫在 a 附近单调不减；两边对调则是单调不增。证明用反证：若导数 L < 0，左侧抬高给出一个 x < a 使 f a < f x，局部单调却给出 f x ≤ f a，两条不等式相撞。非增那一边把左右对调。

边界：只做局部单调，不声称整段区间上的单调；反向的「导数非负推出单调」需要中值定理，本阶段没有。恒等函数在零点附近单调不减却不单调不增，这条读数说明两个方向不能互换。

局部单调

「在 a 附近单调不减」：有一个正半径，邻域内左边的点不超过 a 处的值，右边的点不低于 a 处的值。两条一起写，是因为「不下降」本来就同时管两侧。

定义NondecreasingNear声明 1
def NondecreasingNear (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep) : Prop :=
  ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
    (∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLeRep x a → ratLeRep (f x) (f a)) ∧
    (∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLeRep a x → ratLeRep (f a) (f x))

「在 a 附近单调不增」：左边的点不低于 a 处的值，右边的点不超过 a 处的值。

定义NonincreasingNear声明 2
def NonincreasingNear (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep) : Prop :=
  ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
    (∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLeRep x a → ratLeRep (f a) (f x)) ∧
    (∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLeRep a x → ratLeRep (f x) (f a))
练习 1nondecreasingNear_iff定理附录解答
theorem nondecreasingNear_iff (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep) :
    NondecreasingNear f a ↔
      ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧

        (∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLeRep a x →

  sorry
提示 1思路

定义就是右边那串量词，两个方向都直接返回假设。

练习 3not_risesLeft_of_nondecreasingNear定理附录解答

单调挡住一侧的抬高

theorem not_risesLeft_of_nondecreasingNear (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (h : NondecreasingNear f a) : ¬ RisesLeft f a :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

反证。取单调邻域与抬高邻域的较小半径 d，在 a 左侧取 x = a - d/2。单调给出 f x ≤ f a，抬高给出 f a < f x；反对称性把两条合成等式，与严格小的第二分量冲突。

提示 2目标形态

NondecreasingNear f a → ¬ RisesLeft f a。

提示 3关键件

exists_min_pos · close_sub_half · close_mono_eps · ratLtRep_self_sub_pos · ratLeRep_antisymm · And.right。

查看解答 定理 · 17 行

把局部单调的存在性拆成半径、左侧条件与右侧条件，再把抬高的存在性拆开， 两个半径交给冲突引理。不减只用左侧条件。

theorem not_risesLeft_of_nondecreasingNear (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (h : NondecreasingNear f a) : ¬ RisesLeft f a :=
  fun (hr : RisesLeft f a) =>
    Exists.elim h
      (fun (dm : ratRep)
          (hdm : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dm ∧
            (∀ (x : ratRep), close x a dm → ratLeRep x a →
              ratLeRep (f x) (f a)) ∧
            (∀ (x : ratRep), close x a dm → ratLeRep a x →
              ratLeRep (f a) (f x))) =>
        Exists.elim hr
          (fun (dr : ratRep)
              (hdr : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dr ∧
                ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLtRep x a → close x a dr →
                  ratLtRep (f a) (f x)) =>
            risesLeft_conflict_mono f a dm dr (And.left hdm)
              (And.left (And.right hdm)) (And.left hdr) (And.right hdr)))
练习 4not_risesRight_of_nonincreasingNear定理附录解答
theorem not_risesRight_of_nonincreasingNear (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (a : ratRep) (h : NonincreasingNear f a) : ¬ RisesRight f a :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

与上一条左右对调。这次在 a 右侧取 x = a + d/2；非增给出 f x ≤ f a，抬高给出 f a < f x，同样相撞。

提示 2目标形态

NonincreasingNear f a → ¬ RisesRight f a。

查看解答 定理 · 17 行

不增这一边用右侧条件，其余与上一条对称。

theorem not_risesRight_of_nonincreasingNear (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (h : NonincreasingNear f a) : ¬ RisesRight f a :=
  fun (hr : RisesRight f a) =>
    Exists.elim h
      (fun (dm : ratRep)
          (hdm : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dm ∧
            (∀ (x : ratRep), close x a dm → ratLeRep x a →
              ratLeRep (f a) (f x)) ∧
            (∀ (x : ratRep), close x a dm → ratLeRep a x →
              ratLeRep (f x) (f a))) =>
        Exists.elim hr
          (fun (dr : ratRep)
              (hdr : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dr ∧
                ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLtRep a x → close x a dr →
                  ratLtRep (f a) (f x)) =>
            risesRight_conflict_mono f a dm dr (And.left hdm)
              (And.right (And.right hdm)) (And.left hdr) (And.right hdr)))
练习 5deriv_nonneg_of_nondecreasingNear定理附录解答

导数的符号

theorem deriv_nonneg_of_nondecreasingNear (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (h : HasDerivAt f a L) (hmono : NondecreasingNear f a) :
    ¬ ratLtRep L ratZeroRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

若 L < 0，risesLeft_of_deriv_neg 给出左侧抬高，与单调不减对应的「不抬高」冲突；所以 ¬ L < 0。

提示 2目标形态

HasDerivAt f a L → NondecreasingNear f a → ¬ L < 0。

提示 3关键件

risesLeft_of_deriv_neg · not_risesLeft_of_nondecreasingNear。

查看解答 定理 · 6 行

导数为负就落到左侧抬高，而单调不减不允许左侧抬高；两条一碰，负导数 就被排除。剩下的正是「导数不为负」。

theorem deriv_nonneg_of_nondecreasingNear (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a L : ratRep)
    (h : HasDerivAt f a L) (hmono : NondecreasingNear f a) :
    ¬ ratLtRep L ratZeroRep :=
  fun (hL : ratLtRep L ratZeroRep) =>
    not_risesLeft_of_nondecreasingNear f a hmono
      (risesLeft_of_deriv_neg f a L h hL)

练习 7demo_id_nondecreasingNear定理附录解答

三个读数

theorem demo_id_nondecreasingNear :
    NondecreasingNear (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

半径取 1。两侧的条件经过恒等函数都变成 x ≤ 0 → x ≤ 0 与 0 ≤ x → 0 ≤ x，直接返回假设。

提示 2目标形态

NondecreasingNear (fun x => x) 0。

查看解答 定理 · 9 行

theorem demo_id_nondecreasingNear :
    NondecreasingNear (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep :=
  Exists.intro ratOneRep
    (And.intro ratLtRep_zero_one
      (And.intro
        (fun (x : ratRep) (_hx : close x ratZeroRep ratOneRep)
            (hle : ratLeRep x ratZeroRep) => hle)
        (fun (x : ratRep) (_hx : close x ratZeroRep ratOneRep)
            (hle : ratLeRep ratZeroRep x) => hle)))
