练习 9demo_id_not_nonincreasing定理附录解答
theorem demo_id_not_nonincreasing :
    ¬ NonincreasingNear (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

任取非增邻域的半径 delta，右侧点 0 + delta/2 落在邻域里。非增要求它 ≤ 0，而它严格大于 0；反对称性把两条合成 0 ~ (0 + delta/2)，与严格小的第二分量冲突。

提示 2目标形态

¬ NonincreasingNear (fun x => x) 0。

提示 3关键件

close_add_right · ratLtRep_self_add_pos · ratLeRep_antisymm · And.right。

查看解答 定理 · 24 行

恒等函数不是局部不增：任取半径 delta，右侧点 0 + delta/2 落在邻域里， 非增要求它不超过 0，它却严格大于 0。两条不等式合成 0 ~ (0 + delta/2)， 与严格小的第二分量相撞。

theorem demo_id_not_nonincreasing :
    ¬ NonincreasingNear (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep :=
  fun (h : NonincreasingNear (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep) =>
    Exists.elim h
      (fun (delta : ratRep)
          (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
            (∀ (x : ratRep), close x ratZeroRep delta →
              ratLeRep x ratZeroRep → ratLeRep ratZeroRep x) ∧
            (∀ (x : ratRep), close x ratZeroRep delta →
              ratLeRep ratZeroRep x → ratLeRep x ratZeroRep)) =>
        (And.right (ratLtRep_self_add_pos ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta)
          (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd))))
          (ratLeRep_antisymm ratZeroRep (ratAddRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta))
            (ratLtRep_le ratZeroRep (ratAddRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta))
              (ratLtRep_self_add_pos ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta)
                (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd))))
            ((And.right (And.right hd))
              (ratAddRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta))
              (close_add_right ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta) delta
                (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd))
                (ratHalf_lt_self delta (And.left hd)))
              (ratLtRep_le ratZeroRep (ratAddRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta))
                (ratLtRep_self_add_pos ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta)
                  (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd)))))))

单调与导数的符号：不下降的曲线不会往下斜

练习 9 · demo_id_not_nonincreasing 定理

恒等函数不是局部不增：任取半径 delta，右侧点 0 + delta/2 落在邻域里， 非增要求它不超过 0，它却严格大于 0。两条不等式合成 0 ~ (0 + delta/2)， 与严格小的第二分量相撞。

theorem demo_id_not_nonincreasing :
    ¬ NonincreasingNear (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep :=
  fun (h : NonincreasingNear (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep) =>
    Exists.elim h
      (fun (delta : ratRep)
          (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
            (∀ (x : ratRep), close x ratZeroRep delta →
              ratLeRep x ratZeroRep → ratLeRep ratZeroRep x) ∧
            (∀ (x : ratRep), close x ratZeroRep delta →
              ratLeRep ratZeroRep x → ratLeRep x ratZeroRep)) =>
        (And.right (ratLtRep_self_add_pos ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta)
          (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd))))
          (ratLeRep_antisymm ratZeroRep (ratAddRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta))
            (ratLtRep_le ratZeroRep (ratAddRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta))
              (ratLtRep_self_add_pos ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta)
                (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd))))
            ((And.right (And.right hd))
              (ratAddRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta))
              (close_add_right ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta) delta
                (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd))
                (ratHalf_lt_self delta (And.left hd)))
              (ratLtRep_le ratZeroRep (ratAddRep ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta))
                (ratLtRep_self_add_pos ratZeroRep (ratHalf delta)
                  (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd)))))))
补充 · close_sub_half 定理
theorem close_sub_half (a d : ratRep) (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep d) :
    close (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)) a d :=
  close_of_le_lb (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)) a d (ratHalf d)
    (ratLtRep_le (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)) a
      (ratLtRep_self_sub_pos a (ratHalf d) (ratLtRep_half d hd)))
    (ratLeRep_of_ratRel a (ratAddRep (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)) (ratHalf d))
      (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)) (ratHalf d)) a
        (ratSubRep_add_self a (ratHalf d))))
    (ratHalf_lt_self d hd)
补充 · risesLeft_conflict_mono 定理
theorem risesLeft_conflict_mono (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a dm dr : ratRep)
    (hdm : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dm)
    (hmono : ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a dm → ratLeRep x a →
      ratLeRep (f x) (f a))
    (hdr : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dr)
    (hrise : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLtRep x a → close x a dr →
      ratLtRep (f a) (f x)) :
    False :=
  Exists.elim (exists_min_pos dm dr hdm hdr)
    (fun (d : ratRep)
        (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep d ∧ ratLeRep d dm ∧ ratLeRep d dr) =>
      (And.right (hrise (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d))
        (ratLtRep_self_sub_pos a (ratHalf d)
          (ratLtRep_half d (And.left hd)))
        (close_mono_eps (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)) a d dr
          (close_sub_half a d (And.left hd))
          (And.right (And.right hd)))))
        (ratLeRep_antisymm (f a) (f (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)))
          (And.left (hrise (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d))
            (ratLtRep_self_sub_pos a (ratHalf d)
              (ratLtRep_half d (And.left hd)))
            (close_mono_eps (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)) a d dr
              (close_sub_half a d (And.left hd))
              (And.right (And.right hd)))))
          (hmono (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d))
            (close_mono_eps (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)) a d dm
              (close_sub_half a d (And.left hd))
              (And.left (And.right hd)))
            (ratLtRep_le (ratSubRep a (ratHalf d)) a
              (ratLtRep_self_sub_pos a (ratHalf d)
                (ratLtRep_half d (And.left hd)))))))
补充 · risesRight_conflict_mono 定理
theorem risesRight_conflict_mono (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a dm dr : ratRep)
    (hdm : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dm)
    (hmono : ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a dm → ratLeRep a x →
      ratLeRep (f x) (f a))
    (hdr : ratLtRep ratZeroRep dr)
    (hrise : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLtRep a x → close x a dr →
      ratLtRep (f a) (f x)) :
    False :=
  Exists.elim (exists_min_pos dm dr hdm hdr)
    (fun (d : ratRep)
        (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep d ∧ ratLeRep d dm ∧ ratLeRep d dr) =>
      (And.right (hrise (ratAddRep a (ratHalf d))
        (ratLtRep_self_add_pos a (ratHalf d)
          (ratLtRep_half d (And.left hd)))
        (close_mono_eps (ratAddRep a (ratHalf d)) a d dr
          (close_add_right a (ratHalf d) d (ratLtRep_half d (And.left hd))
            (ratHalf_lt_self d (And.left hd)))
          (And.right (And.right hd)))))
        (ratLeRep_antisymm (f a) (f (ratAddRep a (ratHalf d)))
          (And.left (hrise (ratAddRep a (ratHalf d))
            (ratLtRep_self_add_pos a (ratHalf d)
              (ratLtRep_half d (And.left hd)))
            (close_mono_eps (ratAddRep a (ratHalf d)) a d dr
              (close_add_right a (ratHalf d) d (ratLtRep_half d (And.left hd))
                (ratHalf_lt_self d (And.left hd)))
              (And.right (And.right hd)))))
          (hmono (ratAddRep a (ratHalf d))
            (close_mono_eps (ratAddRep a (ratHalf d)) a d dm
              (close_add_right a (ratHalf d) d (ratLtRep_half d (And.left hd))
                (ratHalf_lt_self d (And.left hd)))
              (And.left (And.right hd)))
            (ratLtRep_le a (ratAddRep a (ratHalf d))
              (ratLtRep_self_add_pos a (ratHalf d)
                (ratLtRep_half d (And.left hd)))))))
